//! Intersect authored authority and reuse an authored old endpoint when a
//! deliberately relaxed unique key invalidates its former point lookup.
// @ref LLP 3000.000.000#20-visible-rollback-views-roll — no invented row choice or broader audience.
use crate::{
    ast,
    lexer::{self, Kind},
    parser, print,
};
use std::collections::{BTreeMap, BTreeSet};
fn offset(text: &str, line: u32, col: u32) -> usize {
    let start=text.split_inclusive('\n').take(line.saturating_sub(1) as usize).map(str::len).sum::<usize>();
    start+text[start..].chars().take(col.saturating_sub(1) as usize).map(char::len_utf8).sum::<usize>()
}
fn line_span(file: &str, text: &str, site: &crate::Site) -> Option<(usize, usize)> {
    let (tokens, _) = lexer::lex(file, text);
    let at = tokens.iter().position(|t| t.site == *site)?;
    let end = tokens[at..].iter().find(|t| t.kind == Kind::Newline)?;
    Some((
        offset(text, site.line, 1),
        offset(text, end.site.line, end.site.col),
    ))
}
fn declaration(text: &str, line: u32) -> (usize, usize, String) {
    let start = offset(text, line, 1);
    let mut end = text.len();
    for (next, s) in text.lines().enumerate().skip(line as usize) {
        if !s.trim().is_empty() && !s.starts_with(char::is_whitespace) && !s.starts_with("--") {
            end = offset(text, next as u32 + 1, 1);
            break;
        }
    }
    (start, end, text[start..end].to_owned())
}
pub(super) fn carry(
    sources: &mut [(String, String)],
    current: &[(String, String)],
    changes: &mut Vec<String>,
    forced_programs: &BTreeSet<String>,
) -> BTreeSet<String> {
    let docs = current
        .iter()
        .map(|(f, t)| (f, t, parser::parse_document(f, t).0))
        .collect::<Vec<_>>();
    let mut aliases = BTreeMap::new();
    let mut tables = BTreeMap::new();
    let mut programs = BTreeMap::new();
    for (file, text, doc) in &docs {
        for table in &doc.tables {
            tables.insert(table.name.clone(), table);
            if let Some(old) = &table.renamed_from {
                aliases.insert(table.name.clone(), old.clone());
                tables.insert(old.clone(), table);
            }
        }
        for p in &doc.programs {
            programs.insert(super::release_bridge::operation_name(file,&p.name), (*file, *text, p));
        }
        for(name,target,_)in &doc.aliases {if let Some(p)=doc.programs.iter().find(|p|p.name==*target){programs.insert(super::release_bridge::operation_name(file,name),(*file,*text,p));}}
    }
    for(file,text)in sources.iter(){for old in parser::parse_document(file,text).0.tables{if let Some(prior)=&old.renamed_from{if !tables.contains_key(&old.name){if let Some(next)=tables.get(prior).copied(){tables.insert(old.name.clone(),next);aliases.insert(prior.clone(),old.name.clone());}}}}}
    let before_schema=crate::compile(sources).schema;
    let current_schema=crate::compile(current).schema.with_table_names(&aliases).ok();
    let remap = |expr: &ast::Expr| {
        let mut expr = expr.clone();
        crate::bind::intent_walk::expression(&mut expr, &mut |e| {
            if let ast::Expr::Read(r)
            | ast::Expr::Exists { read: r, .. }
            | ast::Expr::Created { read: r, .. } = e
            {
                if let Some(old) = aliases.get(&r.table) {
                    r.table = old.clone();
                }
            }
        });
        expr
    };
    let mut relaxed = BTreeSet::new();
    let mut expanded = BTreeSet::new();
    for (file, text) in sources {
        let doc = parser::parse_document(file, text).0;
        let mut edits = Vec::new();
        for old in &doc.tables {
            let Some(next) = tables.get(&old.name) else {
                continue;
            };
            let current_text=docs.iter().find(|(_,_,d)|d.tables.iter().any(|t|t.name==next.name)).map(|(_,text,_)|*text).unwrap();
            let authored_read=|rule:&ast::RuleDecl|->Option<String>{
                if rule.effect!=ast::Effect::Read{return None}
                let(a,b)=line_span(&rule.site.file,current_text,&rule.site)?;let raw=&current_text[a..b];
                raw.trim_start().starts_with("read through ").then(||raw.to_owned())
            };
            // Replacement and insertion must use the same set: strengthening
            // a named ordering to unique cannot leave two declarations behind.
            let columns=super::release_bridge_indexes::column_names(old,next);
            let same_key=|before:&ast::IndexDecl,after:&ast::IndexDecl| {
                let Some(columns)=&columns else{return false};
                if before.is_plain()&&after.is_plain() {
                    return before.columns==after.columns.iter().map(|c|columns.get(c).unwrap_or(c).clone()).collect::<Vec<_>>();
                }
                // Opaque components are never inferred from their printed
                // column advice. Preserve only their full compiled meaning.
                match (before_schema.table(&old.name).and_then(|t|t.index(&before.name)),
                    current_schema.as_ref().and_then(|s|s.table(&old.name)).and_then(|t|t.index(&after.name))) {
                    (Some(before),Some(after))=>before.components==after.components,
                    _=>false,
                }
            };
            let carried_indexes=next.indexes.iter().filter(|i|
                !old.indexes.iter().any(|o|o.name==i.name)
                || old.indexes.iter().any(|o|o.name==i.name&&o.unique!=i.unique)
                || (i.unique&&!old.indexes.iter().any(|o|o.unique&&same_key(o,i)))
                || old.indexes.iter().any(|o|o.name==i.name&&o.unique&&!next.indexes.iter().any(|n|n.unique&&same_key(o,n))))
                .collect::<Vec<_>>();
            for index in &old.indexes {
                let loses_unique=index.unique&&!next.indexes.iter().any(|n|n.unique&&same_key(index,n));
                if !loses_unique&&!carried_indexes.iter().any(|n|n.name==index.name){continue}
                if let Some((start, end)) = line_span(file, text, &index.site) {
                    edits.push((start, end, String::new()));
                    if loses_unique {
                        relaxed.insert(old.name.clone());
                        changes.push(format!("{}: current source permits multiple rows for {}; current authored endpoint supplies the old-client selection",old.name,index.columns.join(", ")));
                    }
                }
            }
            for column in &next.columns {
                if let Some(prior)=old
                    .columns
                    .iter()
                    .find(|c| c.name == column.name || column.renamed_from.as_ref() == Some(&c.name)||c.renamed_from.as_ref()==Some(&column.name))

                        edits.push(edit);
                        changes.push(format!("{}.{}: retained current authored default",old.name,prior.name));
                    }
                    continue;
                }
                let Some((_, current_text, _)) = docs
                    .iter()
                    .find(|(_, _, d)| d.tables.iter().any(|t| t.name == next.name))

                    continue;
                };

                    edits.push((at, at, format!("{}\n", &current_text[a..b])));
                    expanded.insert(old.name.clone());
                    changes.push(format!(
                        "{}.{}: retained current guard/input field",
                        old.name, column.name
                    ));
                }
            }
            if next.public.is_none(){if let Some((_,site))=&old.public {if let Some((a,b))=line_span(file,text,site){edits.push((a,b,String::new()));}}}

                        current.effect.word(),

                    ),

                    old.name,
                    current.effect.word()
                ));
            }
            // Constraints are current authority too: carry authored quotas,
            // immutable fields and final-existence requirements verbatim.
            for (_,site) in &old.immutable {if let Some((a,b))=line_span(file,text,site){edits.push((a,b,String::new()));}}
            for cap in &old.caps {if let Some((a,b))=line_span(file,text,&cap.site){edits.push((a,b,String::new()));}}

            for(expr,_)in &next.final_exists {edits.push((at,at,format!("  after insert <- {}\n",print::expression(&remap(expr)))));}

            if let Some((field,_))=&next.not_before {edits.push((at,at,format!("  not before .{field}\n")));}
            // An authored current endpoint or guard may name a new index. Keep
            // that index with the body; an equal-key old name is not an alias.
            for index in carried_indexes {

                    }

            for rule in &old.rules {

                    })
                    .collect::<Vec<_>>()
                    .join("\n");
                if text[start..end] != rules {
                    edits.push((start, end, rules));
                    changes.push(format!(
                        "{}: current authored {} authority retained",
                        old.name,
                        rule.effect.word()

                    continue;

                edits.push((text.len(), text.len(), format!("\n{source}")));
                changes.push(format!(
                    "{}: retained current migration {}",
                    backfill.table, backfill.name
                ));
                let _ = current_file;
            }
        }
        edits.sort_by_key(|e| std::cmp::Reverse((e.0, e.1)));
        for (a, b, value) in edits {
            text.replace_range(a..b, &value);
        }
    }
    relaxed.extend(expanded);
    relaxed
}

fn current_default(file:&str,text:&str,old:&ast::ColumnDecl,new:&ast::ColumnDecl)->Option<(usize,usize,String)> {
    let authored=|column:&ast::ColumnDecl|column.default.as_ref().map(print::expression);
    if authored(old)==authored(new){return None;}
    let replacement=authored(new).map(|value|format!("default {value}")).unwrap_or_default();
    if let Some(value)=&old.default {
        let(tokens,_)=lexer::lex(file,text);
        let at=tokens.iter().position(|token|token.site==*value.site())?;
        let keyword=at.checked_sub(1).filter(|i|tokens[*i].value=="default")?;
        let end=at+if matches!(value,ast::Expr::Unary{..}){2}else{1};
        let end=offset(text,tokens.get(end)?.site.line,tokens.get(end)?.site.col).min(text.len());
        Some((offset(text,tokens[keyword].site.line,tokens[keyword].site.col),text[..end].trim_end().len(),replacement))
    }else{
        let(_,end)=super::release_bridge::type_span(file,text,old)?;
        Some((end,end,format!(" {replacement}")))
    }
}

pub(super) fn input_accepts(old: &ast::ScalarType, new: &ast::ScalarType) -> bool {
    if old == new {
        return true;
    }
    if old.optional && !new.optional {
        return false;
    }
    use ast::TypeKind::*;
    let bounds = |k: &ast::TypeKind| match k {
        Int => Some((i64::MIN, i64::MAX)),
        IntRange { min, max } => Some((min.unwrap_or(i64::MIN), max.unwrap_or(i64::MAX))),
        _ => None,
    };
    match (bounds(&old.kind), bounds(&new.kind)) {
        (Some((a, b)), Some((x, y))) => x <= a && y >= b,
        _ => match (&old.kind,&new.kind){(Enum{variants:a},Enum{variants:b})=>a.iter().all(|v|b.contains(v)),_=>old.kind==new.kind},
    }
}

#[cfg(test)]
mod tests {
    use crate::release_bridge::loosen;
    #[test]
    fn preserves_current_transition_guard_fields_and_authored_old_client_writes() {
        let old = "table items:\n  n: int 1..10\n  insert <- allow\n  update <- allow\n  online only\nmutation add(n: int 1..10):\n  return insert items {n}\n";
        let current = "table items:\n  n: int 0..10\n  category: enum('free','paid')\n  insert <- allow\n  update (old,next) <- old.category = next.category\n  online only\nmutation add(n: int 0..10):\n  return insert items {n,category:'free' if n = 0 else 'paid'}\nbackfill items classify(n: int 0..10): {category:'free' if n = 0 else 'paid'}\n";
        let bridge = loosen(
            &[("schema.q".into(), old.into())],
            &[("schema.q".into(), current.into())],
        );
        assert!(
            bridge.diagnostics.is_empty(),
            "{:?}\n{:?}",
            bridge.diagnostics,
            bridge.sources
        );
        let text = &bridge.sources[0].1;
        assert!(text.contains("old.category = next.category"));
        assert!(text.contains("category:'free' if n = 0 else 'paid'"));
        assert!(text.contains("backfill items classify"));
    }
    #[test]
    fn keeps_a_fitting_old_write_when_current_guards_transport_without_replacing_it(){
        let old="table items:\n  amount: int\n  insert <- allow\n  online only\nmutation add():\n  return insert items {amount:100}\n";
        let current=old.replace("amount: int", "amount: int ?").replace("amount:100", "amount:null");
        let b=loosen(&[("schema.q".into(),old.into())],&[("schema.q".into(),current)]);assert!(b.diagnostics.is_empty(),"{:?}",b.diagnostics);assert!(b.sources[0].1.contains("amount:100"));
    }
    #[test]
    fn carries_current_guards_even_when_the_join_has_no_changed_columns(){
        let old="table items:\n  n: int\n  insert <- allow\n  online only\nmutation add(n: int):\n  return insert items {n}\n";
        let current=old.replace("  return insert items","  require n > 0 else TOO_EARLY\n  return insert items");
        let b=loosen(&[("schema.q".into(),old.into())],&[("schema.q".into(),current)]);assert!(b.diagnostics.is_empty(),"{:?}",b.diagnostics);assert!(b.sources[0].1.contains("TOO_EARLY"));
    }
    #[test]
    fn does_not_reuse_an_endpoint_that_requires_new_arguments() {
        let old = "table items:\n  n: int\n  insert <- allow\n  online only\nmutation add(n: int):\n  return insert items {n}\n";
        let current = "table items:\n  n: int\n  required: int\n  insert <- allow\n  online only\nmutation add(n: int, required: int):\n  return insert items {n,required}\n";
        let bridge = loosen(
            &[("schema.q".into(), old.into())],
            &[("schema.q".into(), current.into())],
        );
        assert!(!bridge.diagnostics.is_empty());
    }
}

//! Carry authored guards; never invent a value for a newly optional field.
// @ref LLP 3000.000.000#20-visible-rollback-views-roll — compilation gates automatic registration.
use crate::{ast::Stmt, parser, print};
use std::collections::BTreeSet;

// Guard meaning depends on statement order and named definitions, not merely
// the last textual binding. Compare declared row identities, then select
// current execution when that guarded context cannot be transported intact.
// Field/table authority is validated separately before choosing a strategy.
pub(super) fn current_context_needed(
    sources:&[(String,String)],current:&[(String,String)],
    view_schema:&snapback4_core::Schema,current_schema:&snapback4_core::Schema,
)->bool {
    let view_docs=sources.iter().map(|(f,t)|parser::parse_document(f,t).0).collect::<Vec<_>>();
    let mut next_docs=current.iter().map(|(f,t)|parser::parse_document(f,t).0).collect::<Vec<_>>();
    let mut names=current_schema.table_renames.iter().filter(|(_,old)|view_schema.tables.contains_key(*old)).map(|(new,old)|(new.clone(),old.clone())).collect::<std::collections::BTreeMap<_,_>>();
    for(name,prior)in &view_schema.table_renames {
        if current_schema.tables.contains_key(prior)&&!current_schema.tables.contains_key(name){names.insert(prior.clone(),name.clone());}
    }
    for doc in &mut next_docs{normalize_identities(doc,&names);}
    if super::release_bridge_indexes::current_context_needed(&view_docs,&next_docs,view_schema,current_schema,&names){return true;}
    let exports=|docs:&[crate::ast::Document]| {
        let mut out=std::collections::BTreeMap::new();
        for doc in docs {
            for p in &doc.programs{out.insert(super::release_bridge::operation_name(&doc.file,&p.name),p.clone());}
            for(name,target,_)in &doc.aliases{if let Some(p)=doc.programs.iter().find(|p|p.name==*target){out.insert(super::release_bridge::operation_name(&doc.file,name),p.clone());}}
        }out
    };
    let shapes=|docs:&[crate::ast::Document]| {
        let mut out=std::collections::BTreeMap::new();
        for doc in docs {
            for shape in &doc.shapes {
                let fields=shape.fields.iter().map(|field|match field {
                    crate::ast::ShapeField::Spread{..}=>"...".into(),
                    crate::ast::ShapeField::NamedSpread{name,..}=>format!("...{name}"),
                    crate::ast::ShapeField::Bare{name,..}=>name.clone(),
                    crate::ast::ShapeField::Field{name,value,..}=>format!("{name}:{}",expression_signature(value)),
                }).collect::<Vec<_>>();
                out.insert(shape.name.clone(),fields);
            }
        }out
    };
    let shapes_changed=shapes(&view_docs)!=shapes(&next_docs);
    let view=exports(&view_docs);let next=exports(&next_docs);
    for(name,p)in next {
        let Some(old)=view.get(&name)else{continue};
        let mut sensitive=p.prelude.iter().chain(&p.body).any(|s|matches!(s,Stmt::Require{..}|Stmt::If{..}|Stmt::For{..}|Stmt::As{..}));
        let mut doc=crate::ast::Document::default();doc.programs.push(p.clone());
        crate::bind::intent_walk::document(&mut doc,&mut |expr|{
            sensitive|=matches!(expr,crate::ast::Expr::Read(_)|crate::ast::Expr::Exists{..}|crate::ast::Expr::Created{..}|crate::ast::Expr::Walk{..}|crate::ast::Expr::Merge(_)|crate::ast::Expr::If{..}|crate::ast::Expr::Assert{..}|crate::ast::Expr::Call{..}|crate::ast::Expr::Write{condition:Some(_),..});
        });
        let body=|p:&crate::ast::ProgramDecl|p.prelude.iter().chain(&p.body).map(normalized_statement).collect::<Vec<_>>();
        if sensitive&&(body(old)!=body(&p)||shapes_changed){return true;}
    }
    false
}

// Only typed row-reference slots change spelling. Literal values, member names,
// helper identities and business strings retain their authored meaning.
fn normalize_identities(doc:&mut crate::ast::Document,names:&std::collections::BTreeMap<String,String>) {
    fn kind(ty:&mut crate::ast::ScalarType,names:&std::collections::BTreeMap<String,String>){
        match &mut ty.kind {
            crate::ast::TypeKind::Ref{table}=>if let Some(old)=names.get(table){*table=old.clone();},
            crate::ast::TypeKind::List{item,..}=>kind(item,names),
            _=>(),
        }
    }
    for p in &mut doc.programs{for a in &mut p.args{kind(&mut a.type_,names);}}
    for f in &mut doc.functions{for a in &mut f.args{kind(&mut a.type_,names);}}
    let table=|name:&mut String|{if let Some(old)=names.get(name){*name=old.clone();}};
    crate::bind::intent_walk::document(doc,&mut |expr|match expr {
        crate::ast::Expr::Read(r)|crate::ast::Expr::Exists{read:r,..}|crate::ast::Expr::Created{read:r,..}=>table(&mut r.table),
        crate::ast::Expr::Write{table:name,..}|crate::ast::Expr::Walk{table:name,..}=>table(name),
        crate::ast::Expr::Merge(m)=>{table(&mut m.table);for lane in &mut m.lanes{if let Some(name)=&mut lane.table{table(name);}if let Some(r)=&mut lane.planned{table(&mut r.table);}if let Some((_,r))=&mut lane.nulls{table(&mut r.table);}}},
        _=>(),
    });
}

pub(super) fn carry(
    sources: &mut [(String, String)],
    current: &[(String, String)],
    _affected: &BTreeSet<String>,
    changes: &mut Vec<String>,
) -> (Vec<crate::Diagnostic>,BTreeSet<String>) {
    let mut diagnostics = Vec::new();
    let mut fallback=BTreeSet::new();
    let mut current_documents = current
        .iter()
        .map(|(file, text)| parser::parse_document(file, text).0)
        .collect::<Vec<_>>();
    let target_documents = sources
        .iter()
        .map(|(file, text)| parser::parse_document(file, text).0)
        .collect::<Vec<_>>();
    let target_tables=target_documents.iter().flat_map(|d|&d.tables).map(|t|t.name.clone()).collect::<BTreeSet<_>>();
    let current_tables=current_documents.iter().flat_map(|d|&d.tables).map(|t|t.name.clone()).collect::<BTreeSet<_>>();
    let mut identities=std::collections::BTreeMap::new();
    for table in current_documents.iter().flat_map(|d|&d.tables) {
        if let Some(prior)=table.renamed_from.as_ref().filter(|prior|target_tables.contains(*prior)){identities.insert(table.name.clone(),prior.clone());}
    }
    for table in target_documents.iter().flat_map(|d|&d.tables) {
        if let Some(prior)=table.renamed_from.as_ref().filter(|prior|current_tables.contains(*prior)&&!current_tables.contains(&table.name)){identities.insert(prior.clone(),table.name.clone());}
    }
    for doc in &mut current_documents{normalize_identities(doc,&identities);}
    let helper_signatures = |docs: &[crate::ast::Document]| {
        let mut out=std::collections::BTreeMap::new();
        for doc in docs {
            for f in &doc.functions {
                let args=f.args.iter().map(|a|(a.name.clone(),a.type_.clone(),a.alias.clone(),a.default.as_ref().map(expression_signature),a.deprecated)).collect::<Vec<_>>();
                let body=match &f.body {
                    crate::ast::FunctionBody::Expression(e)=>vec![format!("expression {}",expression_signature(e))],
                    crate::ast::FunctionBody::Block(body)=>body.iter().map(normalized_statement).collect(),
                };
                let mut function=crate::ast::Document::default();function.functions.push(f.clone());
                let mut calls=Vec::new();crate::bind::intent_walk::document(&mut function,&mut |e|if let crate::ast::Expr::Call{name,..}=e{calls.push(name.clone());});
                out.insert(f.name.clone(),((args,body),calls));
            }
        }out
    };
    let current_helpers = helper_signatures(&current_documents);
    let target_helpers = helper_signatures(&target_documents);
    let mut next=std::collections::BTreeMap::new();
    for doc in &current_documents {
        for p in &doc.programs {next.insert(super::release_bridge::operation_name(&doc.file,&p.name),p.clone());}
        for(name,target,_)in &doc.aliases {if let Some(p)=doc.programs.iter().find(|p|p.name==*target){let mut exported=p.clone();exported.name=name.clone();next.insert(super::release_bridge::operation_name(&doc.file,name),exported);}}
    }
    for (file, text) in sources {
        let doc = parser::parse_document(file, text).0;
        let mut edits = Vec::new();
        for old in doc.programs {
            let Some(new) = next.get(&super::release_bridge::operation_name(file,&old.name)) else {
                continue;
            };
            // The whole selected source must retain current matching guards,
            // including changes unrelated to the column that triggered a view.
            let guards = old
                .prelude
                .iter()
                .chain(&old.body)
                .filter_map(guard)
                .collect::<BTreeSet<_>>();
            let added = new
                .prelude
                .iter()
                .chain(&new.body)
                .filter_map(guard)
                .filter(|g| !guards.contains(g))
                .collect::<BTreeSet<_>>();
            let bindings = |p: &crate::ast::ProgramDecl| {
                p.prelude
                    .iter()
                    .chain(&p.body)
                    .filter_map(|s| {
                        if let Stmt::Bind { names, value, .. } = s {
                            Some((names, value))
                        } else {
                            None
                        }
                    })
                    .flat_map(|(names, value)| {
                        names.iter().enumerate().map(move |(at, name)| {
                            (
                                name.clone(),
                                (format!("{at}:{}", expression_signature(value)), value.clone()),
                            )
                        })
                    })
                    .collect::<std::collections::BTreeMap<_, _>>()
            };
            let old_bindings = bindings(&old);
            let new_bindings = bindings(new);
            let mut dependent = BTreeSet::new();
            let mut helpers = BTreeSet::new();
            let collect = |expr: &crate::ast::Expr,
                           names: &mut BTreeSet<String>,
                           helpers: &mut BTreeSet<String>| {
                let mut expr = expr.clone();
                crate::bind::intent_walk::expression(&mut expr, &mut |e| match e {
                    crate::ast::Expr::Name { name, .. } => {
                        names.insert(name.clone());
                    }
                    crate::ast::Expr::Call { name, .. } => {
                        helpers.insert(name.clone());
                    }
                    _ => (),
                });
            };
            for s in new.prelude.iter().chain(&new.body) {
                if let Stmt::Require { condition, .. } = s {
                    collect(condition, &mut dependent, &mut helpers);
                }
            }
            let mut visited = BTreeSet::new();
            let mut unsafe_binding = false;
            while let Some(name) = dependent.pop_first() {
                if !visited.insert(name.clone()) {
                    continue;
                }
                if let Some((signature, value)) = new_bindings.get(&name) {
                    if old_bindings.get(&name).map(|(s, _)| s) != Some(signature)
                        || has_write(value)
                    {
                        unsafe_binding = true;
                    }
                    collect(value, &mut dependent, &mut helpers);
                } else if old_bindings.contains_key(&name) {
                    unsafe_binding = true;
                }
            }
            // Reused authored workflows retain their complete helper meaning,
            // including helpers feeding writes, returns and system scopes.
            let mut workflow = crate::ast::Document::default();
            workflow.programs.push(new.clone());
            crate::bind::intent_walk::document(&mut workflow, &mut |e| {
                if let crate::ast::Expr::Call { name, .. } = e {
                    helpers.insert(name.clone());
                }
            });
            let mut visited = BTreeSet::new();
            let mut unsafe_helper = false;
            while let Some(name) = helpers.pop_first() {
                // Helper declarations are global after the compiler validates
                // optional file qualification. Compare the resolved identity.
                let name=name.rsplit('.').next().unwrap_or(&name).to_owned();
                if !visited.insert(name.clone()) {
                    continue;
                }
                if current_helpers.get(&name).map(|s| &s.0)
                    != target_helpers.get(&name).map(|s| &s.0)
                {
                    unsafe_helper = true;
                }
                if let Some((_, calls)) = current_helpers.get(&name) {
                    helpers.extend(calls.iter().cloned());
                }
            }
            let nested = |p: &crate::ast::ProgramDecl| {
                p.body
                    .iter()
                    .filter(|s| matches!(s, Stmt::If { .. } | Stmt::For { .. } | Stmt::As { .. }))
                    .map(|s| normalized_statement(s))
                    .collect::<Vec<_>>()
            };
            if unsafe_binding || unsafe_helper || nested(&old) != nested(new) {
                fallback.insert(super::release_bridge::operation_name(file,&old.name));
                diagnostics.push(crate::diag::type_error(&old.site,format!("rollback guard dependency closure for {} differs; an old binding/helper or control-flow path cannot establish the current guard",old.name),"retain a compatible guarded old workflow or declare intentional hiding of the unrepresentable values").guide("deploy"));
                continue;
            }
            if added.is_empty() {
                continue;
            }
            // Keep the old workflow; add current requirements after its reads,
            // immediately before the first effect/return. Undefined or changed
            // bindings fail ordinary compilation rather than guessing policy.
            let at = old

 succeeded in 0ms:
            let at = old
                .body
                .iter()
                .find(|s| {
                    !matches!(s, Stmt::Require { .. })
                        && !matches!(s,Stmt::Bind{value,..} if !has_write(value))
                })
                .map(stmt_site)
                .unwrap_or(old.site.line + 1);
            let offset = text
                .split_inclusive('\n')
                .take(at.saturating_sub(1) as usize)
                .map(str::len)
                .sum::<usize>();
            edits.push((
                offset,
                added.iter().map(|g| format!("  {g}\n")).collect::<String>(),
            ));
            for g in added {
                changes.push(format!("operation {}: carried current {g}", old.name));
            }
        }
        edits.sort_by_key(|(at, _)| std::cmp::Reverse(*at));
        for (at, guard) in edits {
            text.insert_str(at, &guard);
        }
    }
    (diagnostics,fallback)
}
fn guard(s: &Stmt) -> Option<String> {
    match s {
        Stmt::Require {
            condition,
            code,
            message,
            ..
        } => Some(format!(
            "require {} else {}{}",
            print::expression(condition),
            code,
            message
                .as_ref()
                .map(|m| format!(" '{}'", m.replace('\\', "\\\\").replace('\'', "\\'")))
                .unwrap_or_default()
        )),
        _ => None,
    }
}
fn stmt_site(s: &Stmt) -> u32 {
    match s {
        Stmt::As { site, .. }
        | Stmt::Bind { site, .. }
        | Stmt::Expr { site, .. }
        | Stmt::Require { site, .. }
        | Stmt::Return { site, .. }
        | Stmt::Emit { site, .. }
        | Stmt::If { site, .. }
        | Stmt::For { site, .. }
        | Stmt::Break { site }
        | Stmt::Continue { site } => site.line,
    }
}

fn has_write(e: &crate::ast::Expr) -> bool {
    let mut e = e.clone();
    let mut writes = false;
    crate::bind::intent_walk::expression(&mut e, &mut |e| {
        if matches!(e, crate::ast::Expr::Write { .. }) {
            writes = true;
        }
    });
    writes
}

// A source printer is deliberately lossy (for example it prints a merge's
// header without its lanes). Security comparison retains every AST field and
// strips only diagnostic locations. Exhaustive Expr matching makes new
// expression forms require an explicit location-normalization decision.
fn expression_signature(value:&crate::ast::Expr)->String {
    use crate::ast::{Expr,ShapeField};
    fn shape(fields:&mut [ShapeField]) {
        for field in fields {
            match field {
                ShapeField::Spread{site}|ShapeField::NamedSpread{site,..}|ShapeField::Bare{site,..}|ShapeField::Field{site,..}=>*site=crate::Site::default(),
            }
        }
    }
    fn read(r:&mut crate::ast::ReadExpr) {
        r.site=crate::Site::default();
        for k in &mut r.keys{k.site=crate::Site::default();}
        for seek in &mut r.seeks{seek.site=crate::Site::default();}
        if let Some(range)=&mut r.range{range.site=crate::Site::default();}
    }
    let mut value=value.clone();
    crate::bind::intent_walk::expression(&mut value,&mut |expr| {
        match expr {
            Expr::Read(r)=>read(r),
            Expr::Exists{read:r,site}|Expr::Created{read:r,site}=>{read(r);*site=crate::Site::default();},
            Expr::Merge(m)=>{
                m.site=crate::Site::default();
                for lane in &mut m.lanes {
                    lane.site=crate::Site::default();
                    if let Some(fields)=&mut lane.project{shape(fields);}
                    if let Some(r)=&mut lane.planned{read(r);}
                    if let Some((_,r))=&mut lane.nulls{read(r);}
                }
            },
            Expr::Object{fields,site}=>{*site=crate::Site::default();for f in fields{f.site=crate::Site::default();}},
            Expr::Shape{fields,site,..}=>{*site=crate::Site::default();shape(fields);},
            Expr::Write{keys,fields,site,..}=>{*site=crate::Site::default();for k in keys{k.site=crate::Site::default();}for f in fields{f.site=crate::Site::default();}},
            Expr::Name{site,..}|Expr::Focal{site}|Expr::Literal{site,..}|Expr::Array{site,..}
            |Expr::Unary{site,..}|Expr::Binary{site,..}|Expr::Member{site,..}|Expr::Call{site,..}
            |Expr::Assert{site,..}|Expr::ShapeUse{site,..}|Expr::Walk{site,..}|Expr::If{site,..}=>*site=crate::Site::default(),
        }
    });
    format!("{value:?}")
}

fn normalized_statement(s: &Stmt) -> String {
    match s {
        Stmt::As {
            principal, body, ..
        } => format!(
            "as {} {:?}",
            expression_signature(principal),
            body.iter().map(normalized_statement).collect::<Vec<_>>()
        ),
        Stmt::Bind { names, value, .. } => format!("bind {names:?} {}", expression_signature(value)),
        Stmt::Expr { value, .. } => format!("expr {}", expression_signature(value)),
        Stmt::Require {
            condition,
            code,
            message,
            ..
        } => format!(
            "require {} {code:?} {message:?}",
            expression_signature(condition)
        ),
        Stmt::Return { value, .. } => format!("return {}", expression_signature(value)),
        Stmt::Emit {
            effect,
            payload,
            once,
            ..
        } => format!(
            "emit {effect} {} {:?}",
            expression_signature(payload),
            once.as_ref().map(expression_signature)
        ),
        Stmt::If {
            condition,
            then_body,
            else_body,
            ..
        } => format!(
            "if {} {:?} {:?}",
            expression_signature(condition),
            then_body
                .iter()
                .map(normalized_statement)
                .collect::<Vec<_>>(),
            else_body
                .iter()
                .map(normalized_statement)
                .collect::<Vec<_>>()
        ),
        Stmt::For {
            name, domain, body, ..
        } => format!(
            "for {name} {} {:?}",
            expression_signature(domain),
            body.iter().map(normalized_statement).collect::<Vec<_>>()
        ),
        Stmt::Break { .. } => "break".into(),
        Stmt::Continue { .. } => "continue".into(),
    }
}

//! Independent policy follows the complete authored current execution closure.
// @ref LLP 3000.000.000#20-visible-rollback-views-roll — no restored workflow may bypass current independent intent.
use crate::{
    ast::{self, TypeKind},
    lexer::{self, Kind},
    parser, print,
    release_bridge::Bridge,
};
use snapback4_core::ir::ArgType;
use std::collections::{BTreeMap, BTreeSet};

type Source = (String, String);

pub(super) fn prepare(target: &[Source], current: &[Source], guarded_fallback: &mut bool) -> Bridge {
    *guarded_fallback=false;
    let old = crate::compile(target);
    let next = crate::compile(current);
    if !old.diagnostics.is_empty() {
        return Bridge {
            sources: target.to_vec(),
            changes: vec![],
            diagnostics: old.diagnostics,
        };
    }
    if !next.diagnostics.is_empty() {
        return Bridge {
            sources: current.to_vec(),
            changes: vec![],
            diagnostics: next.diagnostics,
        };
    }
    if contracts(&old, &next) {
        return retained(current.to_vec(), false);
    }
    match alpha(target, current) {
        Ok(Some(sources)) => {
            let bound = crate::compile(&sources);
            if !bound.diagnostics.is_empty() {
                return Bridge {
                    sources,
                    changes: vec![],
                    diagnostics: bound.diagnostics,
                };
            }
            if contracts(&old, &bound) {
                return retained(sources, true);
            }
        }
        Err(error) => {*guarded_fallback=error.unsupported;return refused(current, &error.reason)},
        Ok(None) => {}
    }
    *guarded_fallback=true;
    refused(current,"the complete current workflow does not retain every predecessor table, field, operation and accepted input")
}
fn retained(sources: Vec<Source>, alpha: bool) -> Bridge {
    Bridge {sources,diagnostics:vec![],changes:vec![format!("current independent intent: retained the complete current authored schema, defaults, programs, helpers, jobs and policy declarations{}; predecessor names and accepted inputs remain available; review current results in the old UI",if alpha {" under declared table identity renames"}else{""})]}
}
fn refused(current: &[Source], reason: &str) -> Bridge {
    Bridge {sources:current.to_vec(),changes:vec![],diagnostics:vec![crate::diag::type_error(&crate::Site::default(),format!("rollback view cannot preserve current independent intent: {reason}"),"author a compatible guarded source retaining the predecessor fields and inputs, truthful migrations and the current policy; an acknowledgment does not authorize restoring an unsafe workflow").guide("deploy")]}
}

/// A loosened current workflow may omit a predecessor's nullable, default-free
/// field rather than invent its value. All current schema and executable
/// declarations stay intact; old wire aliases are the only source additions.
pub(super) fn guarded(target:&[Source],current:&[Source])->Bridge {
    let normalized=match alpha(target,current) {
        Ok(sources)=>sources,
        Err(error)=>return refused(current,&error.reason),
    };
    let current=normalized.as_deref().unwrap_or(current);
    let old=crate::compile(target);let next=crate::compile(current);
    if !old.diagnostics.is_empty()||!next.diagnostics.is_empty(){return refused(current,"both authored sources must compile before preparing a guarded view");}
    if !table_contracts(&old,&next,true){return refused(current,"the guarded current workflow needs every predecessor table and required/defaulted field; author a truthful reverse projection or compatible guarded source");}
    let mut sources=current.to_vec();let mut changes=vec!["guarded loosened view: retained the complete current schema, defaults, helpers, programs, jobs, aliases and independent intent; omitted nullable default-free predecessor fields are absent results, never invented values; review current results in the old UI".into()];
    if normalized.is_some(){changes.push("declared table identities: complete current guarded workflow retained under predecessor spellings, without rewriting business strings or weakening authority".into());}
    for(file,text)in &mut sources {
        let doc=parser::parse_document(file,text).0;
        let Some((prior_file,prior_text))=target.iter().find(|(f,_)|super::release_bridge::operation_name(f,"")==super::release_bridge::operation_name(file,""))else{continue};
        let prior=parser::parse_document(prior_file,prior_text).0;
        let(tokens,_)=lexer::lex(file,text);let mut edits=Vec::new();let mut extra=String::new();let mut restored=BTreeMap::new();
        // Retain a missing old exported alias only to an authored current
        // endpoint (including an explicitly authored current alias target).
        // Existing/rebound current aliases remain exactly current-authored.
        for(name,target,_)in &prior.aliases {
            let qualified=super::release_bridge::operation_name(file,name);
            if next.programs.iter().any(|p|p.program.name==qualified){continue;}
            let endpoint=doc.programs.iter().find(|p|p.name==*target).or_else(||doc.aliases.iter().find(|(alias,_,_)|alias==target).and_then(|(_,target,_)|doc.programs.iter().find(|p|p.name==*target)));
            if let Some(endpoint)=endpoint{extra.push_str(&format!("alias {name} = {}\n",endpoint.name));restored.insert(qualified,endpoint.name.clone());}
        }
        for previous in &old.programs {
            let qualified=&previous.program.name;
            let endpoint=doc.programs.iter().find(|p|super::release_bridge::operation_name(file,&p.name)==*qualified).or_else(|| {
                let target=doc.aliases.iter().find(|(name,_,_)|super::release_bridge::operation_name(file,name)==*qualified).map(|(_,target,_)|target).or_else(||restored.get(qualified))?;
                doc.programs.iter().find(|p|p.name==*target)
            });
            let Some(endpoint)=endpoint else{continue};
            let actual=super::release_bridge::operation_name(file,&endpoint.name);
            let Some(current)=next.programs.iter().find(|p|p.program.name==*qualified||p.program.name==actual)else{continue};
            for(alias,owner)in &previous.program.arg_aliases {
                if current.program.argument(alias).is_some(){continue;}
                let Some((canonical,_))=current.program.argument(owner)else{continue};
                let Some(arg)=endpoint.args.iter().find(|arg|arg.name==canonical&&arg.alias.is_none())else{continue};
                if let Ok((_,end))=arg_type(text,&tokens,arg){edits.push((end,end,format!(" alias {alias}")));changes.push(format!("operation {qualified}: retained wire alias {alias} on {canonical}"));}
            }
        }
        edits.sort();edits.dedup();edits.sort_by_key(|edit|std::cmp::Reverse(edit.0));
        for(start,end,value)in edits{text.replace_range(start..end,&value);}
        if !extra.is_empty(){if !text.ends_with('\n'){text.push('\n');}text.push_str(&extra);}
    }
    let bound=crate::compile(&sources);
    if !bound.diagnostics.is_empty(){return Bridge{sources,changes,diagnostics:bound.diagnostics};}
    if !table_contracts(&old,&bound,true)||!endpoint_contracts(&old,&bound,true){return refused(current,"the guarded current workflow cannot retain a predecessor query or a retained export's wire aliases and distinct inputs; author a compatible guarded endpoint with the old accepted arguments");}
    for(name,table)in &old.schema.tables {
        for column in table.columns.keys().filter(|column|!bound.schema.tables[name].columns.contains_key(*column)) {
            if let Some(current)=renamed_column(&old,&bound,name,column){changes.push(format!("{name}.{column}: declared identity is now {name}.{current}; guarded view uses the current spelling and result semantics; review the old UI"));}
            else{changes.push(format!("{name}.{column}: removed nullable default-free field remains absent in this guarded view; historical cells stay retained; review the old UI result"));}
        }
    }
    for previous in &old.programs {
        if !bound.programs.iter().any(|p|p.program.name==previous.program.name){changes.push(format!("operation {}: paused because no compatible current authored write endpoint exists; old calls return E_OP; author a guarded compatibility endpoint if this old workflow is still needed",previous.program.name));}
    }
    Bridge{sources,changes,diagnostics:vec![]}
}

pub(super) fn contracts(old:&crate::Bound,new:&crate::Bound)->bool {
    table_contracts(old,new,false)&&endpoint_contracts(old,new,false)
}
fn table_contracts(old:&crate::Bound,new:&crate::Bound,nullable_gaps:bool)->bool {
    old.schema.tables.iter().all(|(name,table)|new.schema.tables.get(name).is_some_and(|next| {
        table.columns.iter().all(|(column,kind)|next.columns.contains_key(column)
            || (nullable_gaps&&renamed_column(old,new,name,column).is_some())
            || (nullable_gaps&&kind.is_optional()&&!table.defaults.contains_key(column)&&!table.engine_field(column)&&!table.maintained.contains_key(column)))
    }))
}
fn endpoint_contracts(old:&crate::Bound,new:&crate::Bound,pause_removed_writes:bool)->bool {
    old.programs.iter().all(|p|new.programs.iter().find(|n|n.program.name==p.program.name)
        .map_or_else(||pause_removed_writes&&matches!(p.program.kind,snapback4_core::ir::ProgramKind::Mutation|snapback4_core::ir::ProgramKind::Job|snapback4_core::ir::ProgramKind::Action),|n|endpoint_contract(&p.program,&n.program)))
}
pub(super) fn endpoint_contract(old:&snapback4_core::Program,new:&snapback4_core::Program)->bool {
    if old.kind!=new.kind{return false;}
    let mut positions=BTreeSet::new();
    for(name,kind)in &old.args {
        let Some((target,next))=new.argument(name)else{return false};
        if !accepts(kind,next)||!positions.insert(target){return false;}
        if !old.arg_aliases.iter().filter(|(_,owner)|*owner==name).all(|(alias,_)|new.argument(alias).is_some_and(|(canonical,next)|canonical==target&&accepts(kind,next))){return false;}
    }
    new.args.iter().all(|(name,kind)|positions.contains(name.as_str())||matches!(kind,ArgType::Optional(_)))
}
fn renamed_column<'a>(old:&crate::Bound,new:&'a crate::Bound,table:&str,column:&str)->Option<&'a str> {
    let next=new.schema.table(table)?;
    if let Some((name,_))=new.schema.asset_renames.get(table).into_iter().flatten().find(|(name,prior)|prior.as_str()==column&&next.columns.contains_key(*name)){return Some(name.as_str());}
    if let Some(prior)=old.schema.asset_renames.get(table).and_then(|m|m.get(column)) {
        if let Some((name,_))=next.columns.get_key_value(prior){return Some(name.as_str());}
    }
    for transform in &new.schema.backfills {
        if transform.table==table&&transform.inputs.len()==1&&transform.inputs.contains_key(column)&&transform.outputs.len()==1 {
            let name=transform.outputs.iter().next()?;
            if transform.name==format!("$rename:up:{column}:{name}")&&next.columns.contains_key(name){return Some(name.as_str());}
        }
    }
    for transform in &old.schema.backfills {
        if transform.table==table&&transform.outputs.len()==1&&transform.outputs.contains(column)&&transform.inputs.len()==1 {
            let prior=transform.inputs.keys().next()?;
            if transform.name==format!("$rename:up:{prior}:{column}") {
                if let Some((name,_))=next.columns.get_key_value(prior){return Some(name.as_str());}
            }
        }
    }
    None
}
// This is inclusion of wire domains for an unchanged authored current body,
// not synthesis of a conversion or permission to reinterpret old arguments.
fn accepts(old: &ArgType, new: &ArgType) -> bool {
    if old == new {
        return true;
    }
    match (old, new) {
        (ArgType::Optional(a), ArgType::Optional(b)) => accepts(a, b),
        (_, ArgType::Optional(b)) => accepts(old, b),
        (ArgType::Optional(_), _) => false,
        (
            ArgType::Text {
                min: a,
                max: b,
                format: f,
            },
            ArgType::Text {
                min: x,
                max: y,
                format: g,
            },
        ) => f == g && x <= a && b <= y,
        (ArgType::Enum { variants: a }, ArgType::Enum { variants: b }) => {
            a.iter().all(|v| b.contains(v))
        }
        (
            ArgType::List { .. } | ArgType::ListRange { .. },
            ArgType::List { .. } | ArgType::ListRange { .. },
        ) => {
            let parts = |kind: &ArgType| match kind {
                ArgType::List { of, max } => (0, *max, of.clone()),
                ArgType::ListRange { of, min, max } => (*min, *max, of.clone()),
                _ => unreachable!(),
            };
            let (a, b, element) = parts(old);
            let (x, y, next) = parts(new);
            x <= a
                && b <= y
                && match (element, next) {
                    (None, None) => true,
                    (Some(a), Some(b)) => accepts(&a, &b),
                    _ => false,
                }
        }
        (ArgType::IntRange { .. }, ArgType::Int) => true,
        (ArgType::IntRange { min: a, max: b }, ArgType::IntRange { min: x, max: y }) => {
            x <= a && b <= y
        }
        _ => false,
    }
}
fn word(name: &str) -> bool {
    !name.is_empty() && name.chars().all(|c| c.is_ascii_alphanumeric() || c == '_')
}
fn offset(text: &str, line: u32, col: u32) -> usize {
    let start = text
        .split_inclusive('\n')
        .take(line.saturating_sub(1) as usize)
        .map(str::len)
        .sum::<usize>();
    start
        + text[start..]
            .chars()
            .take(col.saturating_sub(1) as usize)
            .map(char::len_utf8)
            .sum::<usize>()
}
fn declaration(text: &str, line: u32) -> (usize, usize) {
    let start = offset(text, line, 1);
    let end = text
        .split_inclusive('\n')
        .enumerate()
        .skip(line as usize)
        .find(|(_, s)| {
            !s.trim().is_empty() && !s.starts_with(char::is_whitespace) && !s.starts_with("--")
        })
        .map(|(i, _)| offset(text, i as u32 + 1, 1))
        .unwrap_or(text.len());
    (start, end)
}
fn remap_type(kind: &mut ast::ScalarType, names: &BTreeMap<String, String>) {
    match &mut kind.kind {
        TypeKind::Ref { table } => {
            if let Some(name) = names.get(table) {
                *table = name.clone()
            }
        }
        TypeKind::List { item, .. } => remap_type(item, names),
        _ => (),
    }
}
// Closed, capture-avoiding alpha conversion of a single source module. Complex
// declaration families require an explicitly authored compatible source.
struct MappingError {reason:String,unsupported:bool}
impl From<String> for MappingError {
    fn from(reason:String)->Self{Self{reason,unsupported:false}}
}
impl From<&str> for MappingError {
    fn from(reason:&str)->Self{reason.to_owned().into()}
}

    let doc = parser::parse_document(file, text).0;
    if old.tables.iter().chain(&doc.tables).any(|t| !word(&t.name)) {
        return Ok(None);
    }

fn source(text: &str) -> Vec<(String, String)> { vec![("schema.q".into(), text.into())] }
fn rows(bridge: &crate::release_bridge::Bridge, values: &[(&str, i64, i64)], query: &str) -> Vec<String> {
    let bound = crate::compile(&bridge.sources);
    assert!(bound.diagnostics.is_empty(), "{:?}", bound.diagnostics);
    let mut engine = Engine::memory(bound.schema, bound.programs.into_iter().map(|p| p.program)).unwrap();
    engine.seed(values.iter().map(|(id, n, rank)| ("notes".into(), Value::Row([
        ("id".into(), Value::Id((*id).into())), ("n".into(), Value::Int(*n)),
        ("rank".into(), Value::Int(*rank)), ("amount".into(), Value::Int(5)),
    ].into_iter().collect())))).unwrap();
    let result = engine.query(query, Context::new("alice", Default::default())).unwrap();
    let Value::Array(rows) = result.data else { panic!("expected rows") };
    rows.into_iter().map(|r| match r.field("id").unwrap() { Value::Id(id) => id.clone(), _ => panic!("id") }).collect()
}
#[test]
fn current_index_is_carried_with_its_endpoint() {
    let old = "table notes:\n  n: int\n  memo: text <=20 ?\n  by byN: n, id\n  public\n  online only\ntable retired:\n  value: int\n  public\n  online only\nquery ordered():\n  return notes first 10 by byN\n";
    let current = "table notes:\n  n: int\n  count: int default 7\n  by byCount: count, id\n  public\n  online only\ntable added:\n  value: int\n  public\n  online only\nquery ordered():\n  return notes where .count = 7 first 10 by byCount\n";
    let bridge = crate::release_bridge::loosen(&source(old), &source(current));
    assert!(bridge.diagnostics.is_empty(), "{:?}", bridge.diagnostics);
    let bound = crate::compile(&bridge.sources);
    assert!(bound.schema.tables["notes"].indexes.contains_key("byN"));
    assert_eq!(bound.schema.tables["notes"].indexes["byCount"].columns(), ["count", "id"]);
    let mut engine = Engine::memory(bound.schema, bound.programs.into_iter().map(|p| p.program)).unwrap();
    engine.seed([("a", 2), ("b", 1)].map(|(id, n)| ("notes".into(), Value::Row([
        ("id".into(), Value::Id(id.into())), ("n".into(), Value::Int(n)),
    ].into_iter().collect())))).unwrap();
    let result = engine.query("ordered", Context::new("alice", Default::default())).unwrap();
    let Value::Array(rows) = result.data else { panic!("expected rows") };
    assert_eq!(rows.iter().map(|r| r.field("n").unwrap().clone()).collect::<Vec<_>>(), [Value::Int(2), Value::Int(1)]);
}

#[test]
fn plain_index_transport_keeps_index_label_and_business_literals() {
    let old = crate::parser::parse_document("schema.q", "table notes:\n  owner: principal\n  online only\n").0;
    let raw = "  unique customer: customer else TAKEN 'customer stays customer'";
    let text = format!("table notes:\n  customer: principal renamed from owner\n{raw}\n  online only\n");
    let current = crate::parser::parse_document("schema.q", &text).0;
    let next = &current.tables[0];
    let result = crate::release_bridge_indexes::plain_declaration("schema.q", raw, &next.indexes[0], &old.tables[0], next);
    assert_eq!(result, "  unique customer: owner else TAKEN 'customer stays customer'");
}

#[test]
fn unused_equivalent_unique_names_do_not_make_implicit_old_lookups_ambiguous() {
    let old="table notes:\n  n: int\n  code: text <=20\n  unique byCode: code\n  public\n  online only\ntable retired:\n  value: int\n  public\n  online only\nquery lookup(code: text <=20):\n  return notes[code]\n";
    let current=old.replace("n: int", "n: int ?").replace("unique byCode:","unique byCurrentCode:")
        .replace("table retired:\n  value: int\n  public\n  online only\n", "");
    let bridge=crate::release_bridge::loosen(&source(old),&source(&current));
    assert!(bridge.diagnostics.is_empty(),"{:?}",bridge.diagnostics);
    let bound=crate::compile(&bridge.sources);
    let mut engine=Engine::memory(bound.schema,bound.programs.into_iter().map(|p|p.program)).unwrap();
    engine.seed([("notes".into(),Value::Row([("id".into(),Value::Id("kept".into())),("n".into(),Value::Int(1)),("code".into(),Value::String("a".into()))].into_iter().collect()))]).unwrap();
    let answer=engine.query("lookup",Context::new("alice",[("code".into(),Value::String("a".into()))].into_iter().collect())).unwrap();
    assert_eq!(answer.data.field("id").unwrap(),&Value::Id("kept".into()));
}

#[test]
fn explicitly_used_equivalent_unique_names_keep_both_workflows() {
    let text="table notes:\n  code: text <=20\n  unique byA: code\n  unique byB: code\n  public\n  online only\nquery a(code: text <=20):\n  return notes[code] by byA\nquery b(code: text <=20):\n  return notes[code] by byB\n";
    let mut view=source(text);
    crate::release_bridge_indexes::prune_unused_equivalent_keys(&mut view,&source(text),&mut Vec::new());
    let bound=crate::compile(&view);assert!(bound.diagnostics.is_empty(),"{:?}",bound.diagnostics);
    assert!(bound.schema.tables["notes"].indexes.contains_key("byA"));
    assert!(bound.schema.tables["notes"].indexes.contains_key("byB"));
    let mut engine=Engine::memory(bound.schema,bound.programs.into_iter().map(|p|p.program)).unwrap();
    engine.seed([("notes".into(),Value::Row([("id".into(),Value::Id("kept".into())),("code".into(),Value::String("a".into()))].into_iter().collect()))]).unwrap();
    for op in ["a","b"] {
        let answer=engine.query(op,Context::new("alice",[("code".into(),Value::String("a".into()))].into_iter().collect())).unwrap();
        assert_eq!(answer.data.field("id").unwrap(),&Value::Id("kept".into()));
    }
}

#[test]
fn unused_keys_with_different_ordered_meanings_remain_ambiguous() {
    for key in ["a", "b, a"] {
        let pair=key.contains(',');
        let text=format!("table notes:\n  a: text <=20\n  b: text <=20\n  unique byA: {}\n  unique byB: {key}\n  public\n  online only\nquery lookup(a: text <=20, b: text <=20):\n  return notes[{}]\n",if pair {"a, b"}else{"b"},if pair {"a, b"}else{"a"});
        let mut view=source(&text);
        crate::release_bridge_indexes::prune_unused_equivalent_keys(&mut view,&source(&text),&mut Vec::new());
        assert!(crate::compile(&view).diagnostics.iter().any(|d|d.code=="E_QUARRY_POSITIONAL_SCAN"));
        assert_eq!(view,source(&text));
    }
}

        .replace("query legacyLookup(code: text <=20):\n  return codes[code] by byCode\n", "");

    assert!(bridge.diagnostics.is_empty(),"{:?}",bridge.diagnostics);
    let bound=crate::compile(&bridge.sources);
    let mut engine=Engine::memory(bound.schema,bound.programs.into_iter().map(|p|p.program)).unwrap();
    use snapback4_core::value::object;
    engine.seed([
        ("codes".into(),object([("id",Value::Id("code".into())),("code",Value::String("a".into())),("n",Value::Int(1))])),
        ("records".into(),object([("id",Value::Id("visible".into())),("value",Value::String("a".into())),("secret",Value::String("permitted".into()))])),
        ("records".into(),object([("id",Value::Id("redacted".into())),("value",Value::String("b".into())),("secret",Value::String("hidden".into()))])),
    ]).unwrap();

    let json=snapback4_core::wire::to_json(&answer.data).to_string();
    assert!(json.contains("permitted"),"{json}");assert!(!json.contains("hidden"),"{json}");
}

//! Compare final-existence obligations in declared row/column identity space.
// @ref LLP 3000.000.000#20-visible-rollback-views-roll — spelling-only changes must not trigger a view.
use std::collections::{BTreeMap, BTreeSet};
use snapback4_core::schema::{Constraint, RuleTerm, Schema};
use crate::parser;

#[derive(Default)]
struct Identities {
    tables: BTreeMap<String, String>,
    columns: BTreeMap<String, BTreeMap<String, String>>,
}
impl Identities {
    fn table(&self, name: &str) -> String {self.tables.get(name).cloned().unwrap_or_else(||name.into())}
    fn column(&self, table: &str, name: &str) -> String {
        self.columns.get(table).and_then(|c|c.get(name)).cloned().unwrap_or_else(||name.into())
    }
    fn term(&self, source: &str, term: &RuleTerm) -> RuleTerm {
        match term {
            RuleTerm::Field(c)=>RuleTerm::Field(self.column(source,c)),
            RuleTerm::OldField(c)=>RuleTerm::OldField(self.column(source,c)),
            RuleTerm::NextField(c)=>RuleTerm::NextField(self.column(source,c)),
            RuleTerm::Reference{table,column,field,old}=>RuleTerm::Reference{
                table:self.table(table),column:self.column(source,column),field:self.column(table,field),old:*old},
            RuleTerm::ReferenceChain{column,hops,old}=>RuleTerm::ReferenceChain{
                column:self.column(source,column),old:*old,hops:hops.iter().map(|h|snapback4_core::schema::RuleHop{
                    table:self.table(&h.table),field:self.column(&h.table,&h.field)}).collect()},
            RuleTerm::Viewer|RuleTerm::Literal(_)=>term.clone(),
        }
    }
    fn signature(&self,schema:&Schema,source:&str,constraint:&Constraint)->Option<(String,Vec<String>,Vec<RuleTerm>)> {
        let Constraint::FinalExistsOnInsert{table,index,key}=constraint else{return None};
        let index=schema.table(table)?.index(index)?;
        if !index.is_plain(){return None;}
        Some((self.table(table),index.columns().iter().map(|c|self.column(table,c)).collect(),
            key.iter().map(|t|self.term(source,t)).collect()))
    }
}

pub(super) fn relaxed(target:&[(String,String)],current:&[(String,String)])->bool {
    let old=target.iter().flat_map(|(f,t)|parser::parse_document(f,t).0.tables).collect::<Vec<_>>();
    if !old.iter().any(|t|!t.final_exists.is_empty()){return false;}
    let next=current.iter().flat_map(|(f,t)|parser::parse_document(f,t).0.tables).collect::<Vec<_>>();
    let (before,after)=(crate::compile(target),crate::compile(current));
    if !before.diagnostics.is_empty()||!after.diagnostics.is_empty(){return true;}
    let mut identities=Identities::default();
    let mut pairs=Vec::new();
    for prior in &old {
        let Some(now)=next.iter().find(|n|n.name==prior.name||n.renamed_from.as_ref()==Some(&prior.name)||prior.renamed_from.as_ref()==Some(&n.name))else{continue};
        identities.tables.insert(now.name.clone(),prior.name.clone());
        let mut columns=BTreeMap::new();
        for c in &prior.columns {
            if let Some(n)=now.columns.iter().find(|n|n.name==c.name||n.renamed_from.as_ref()==Some(&c.name)||c.renamed_from.as_ref()==Some(&n.name)) {
                columns.insert(n.name.clone(),c.name.clone());
            }
        }
        if now.columns.iter().map(|c|columns.get(&c.name).unwrap_or(&c.name)).collect::<BTreeSet<_>>().len()!=now.columns.len(){return true;}
        identities.columns.insert(now.name.clone(),columns);
        pairs.push((prior.name.as_str(),now.name.as_str()));
    }
    // Never let two independent current identities collapse to one old name.
    if next.iter().map(|t|identities.table(&t.name)).collect::<BTreeSet<_>>().len()!=next.len(){return true;}
    let unchanged=Identities::default();
    for (prior,now) in pairs {
        let (Some(old_table),Some(new_table))=(before.schema.table(prior),after.schema.table(now))else{return true};
        for constraint in &old_table.constraints {
            if !matches!(constraint,Constraint::FinalExistsOnInsert{..}){continue;}
            let Some(expected)=unchanged.signature(&before.schema,prior,constraint)else{return true};
            if !new_table.constraints.iter().any(|c|identities.signature(&after.schema,now,c).as_ref()==Some(&expected)){return true;}
        }
    }
    false
}

 succeeded in 0ms:
//! Mutable syntax traversal shared by planning and final index-name resolution.
use crate::ast::*;

pub(crate) fn expression(value: &mut Expr, visit: &mut impl FnMut(&mut Expr)) {
    match value {
        Expr::Read(read) | Expr::Exists { read, .. } | Expr::Created { read, .. } => read_children(read, visit),
        Expr::Unary { value, .. } | Expr::Member { base: value, .. } | Expr::Assert { value, .. } | Expr::ShapeUse { base: value, .. } => expression(value, visit),
        Expr::Binary { left, right, .. } => { expression(left, visit); expression(right, visit); }
        Expr::Array { items, .. } | Expr::Call { args: items, .. } => { for item in items { expression(item, visit); } }
        Expr::Object { fields, .. } => { for field in fields { if let Some(value) = &mut field.value { expression(value, visit); } } }
        Expr::Shape { base, fields, .. } => { expression(base, visit); shape(fields, visit); }
        Expr::If { then, condition, otherwise, .. } => { expression(then, visit); expression(condition, visit); expression(otherwise, visit); }
        Expr::Walk { seed, .. } => expression(seed, visit),
        Expr::Write { keys, fields, condition, .. } => {
            for key in keys { expression(&mut key.value, visit); }
            for field in fields { if let Some(value) = &mut field.value { expression(value, visit); } }
            if let Some(value) = condition { expression(value, visit); }
        }
        Expr::Merge(merge) => {
            for value in [&mut merge.matching, &mut merge.filter, &mut merge.cursor].into_iter().flatten() { expression(value, visit); }
            let mut lanes = Vec::new();
            for mut lane in std::mem::take(&mut merge.lanes) {
                if let Some(predicate) = &mut lane.admission { expression(predicate, visit); }
                if let Some((_, read)) = &mut lane.nulls {
                    let mut value = Expr::Read(*read.clone());
                    expression(&mut value, visit);
                    let Expr::Read(planned) = value else { unreachable!("planned null scan") };
                    **read = planned;
                }
                if let Some(value) = &mut lane.distinct { expression(value, visit); }
                if let Some(fields) = &mut lane.project { shape(fields, visit); }
                for value in &mut lane.prefix { expression(value, visit); }
                if let Some((_, value)) = &mut lane.loop_ { expression(value, visit); }
                if let Some(read) = &mut lane.planned {
                    let mut value = Expr::Read(*read.clone());
                    expression(&mut value, visit);
                    match value {
                        Expr::Read(planned) => **read = planned,
                        Expr::Merge(nested) if lane.loop_.is_none() => {
                            for mut child in nested.lanes {
                                child.branch = lane.branch.clone();
                                child.project = lane.project.clone();
                                lanes.push(child);
                            }
                            continue;
                        }
                        _ => unreachable!("a planned lane is a read or an expanded read"),
                    }
                }
                lanes.push(lane);
            }
            merge.lanes = lanes;
        }
        Expr::Name { .. } | Expr::Focal { .. } | Expr::Literal { .. } => {}
    }
    visit(value);
}

fn read_children(read: &mut ReadExpr, visit: &mut impl FnMut(&mut Expr)) {
    for key in &mut read.keys { expression(&mut key.value, visit); }
    for seek in &mut read.seeks { expression(&mut seek.value, visit); }
    for value in [&mut read.filter, &mut read.matching].into_iter().flatten() { expression(value, visit); }
    if let Some(value) = read.range.as_mut().and_then(|range| range.cursor.as_mut()) { expression(value, visit); }
}

pub(super) fn shape(fields: &mut [ShapeField], visit: &mut impl FnMut(&mut Expr)) {
    for field in fields { if let ShapeField::Field { value, .. } = field { expression(value, visit); } }
}

pub(super) fn statements(body: &mut [Stmt], visit: &mut impl FnMut(&mut Expr)) {
    for stmt in body {
        match stmt {
            Stmt::Bind { value, .. } | Stmt::Expr { value, .. } | Stmt::Return { value, .. } => expression(value, visit),
            Stmt::Require { condition, .. } => expression(condition, visit),
            Stmt::Emit { payload, once, .. } => {
                expression(payload, visit);
                if let Some(key) = once { expression(key, visit); }
            }
            Stmt::If { condition, then_body, else_body, .. } => { expression(condition, visit); statements(then_body, visit); statements(else_body, visit); }
            Stmt::As { principal: domain, body, .. } | Stmt::For { domain, body, .. } => { expression(domain, visit); statements(body, visit); }
            Stmt::Break { .. } | Stmt::Continue { .. } => {}
        }
    }
}

pub(crate) fn document(doc: &mut Document, visit: &mut impl FnMut(&mut Expr)) {
    // @ref LLP 3000.000.000.002#8-wave-21-release-evolution — historical lookups receive the same predicate/index planning as ordinary reads.
    for fill in doc.backfills.iter_mut().chain(&mut doc.downfills) {expression(&mut fill.value,visit);}

    for program in &mut doc.programs { statements(&mut program.prelude, visit); statements(&mut program.body, visit); }
    for function in &mut doc.functions {
        match &mut function.body { FunctionBody::Expression(value) => expression(value, visit), FunctionBody::Block(body) => statements(body, visit) }
    }
    for decl in &mut doc.shapes { shape(&mut decl.fields, visit); }
}

            ast::Expr::Read(r)
            | ast::Expr::Exists { read: r, .. }
            | ast::Expr::Created { read: r, .. } => (&r.table, &r.site),
            ast::Expr::Write { table, site, .. } => (&*table, &*site),
            _ => return,
        };

        if let Some(token) = tokens.iter().find(|t| {
            t.site.line == site.line
                && t.site.col >= site.col
                && t.kind == Kind::Word
                && t.value == *table
        }) {
            let a = offset(text, token.site.line, token.site.col);
            edits.push((a, a + token.value.len(), name.clone()));
        } else {
            reference_error = Some("a table reference has no unambiguous source span".to_owned());
        }
    });
    if let Some(error) = reference_error {
        return Err(error.into());
    }
    for arg in doc
        .programs
        .iter()
        .flat_map(|p| &p.args)
        .chain(doc.functions.iter().flat_map(|p| &p.args))
        .chain(doc.actions.iter().flat_map(|p| &p.args))
        .chain(doc.actions.iter().flat_map(|p| p.returns.iter().flatten()))
    {
        let mut kind = arg.type_.clone();
        remap_type(&mut kind, &names);
        if kind != arg.type_ {
            let (a, b) = arg_type(text, &tokens, arg)?;
            edits.push((a, b, print::scalar_type(&kind)));
        }
    }
    edits.sort_by_key(|(a, b, _)| (*a, *b));
    edits.dedup();
    if edits.iter().any(|(a, b, _)| {
        a > b || *b > text.len() || !text.is_char_boundary(*a) || !text.is_char_boundary(*b)
    }) {
        return Err("an unsupported source span needs an authored compatible mapping".into());
    }
    if edits.windows(2).any(|p| p[0].1 > p[1].0) {
        return Err("overlapping source spans in table identity mapping".into());
    }
    let mut result = text.clone();
    for (a, b, value) in edits.into_iter().rev() {
        result.replace_range(a..b, &value);
    }
    Ok(Some(vec![(file.clone(), result)]))
}
fn header(
    file: &str,
    source: &str,
    from: &str,
    to: &str,
    prior: Option<&str>,
) -> Result<String, String> {
    if prior.is_some_and(|p| !word(p)) {
        return Err("qualified predecessor table origin needs an authored source".into());
    }
    let line = source.lines().next().ok_or("missing table header")?;
    let (tokens, _) = lexer::lex(file, line);
    let name = tokens
        .get(1)
        .filter(|t| t.kind == Kind::Word && t.value == from)
        .ok_or("missing table name span")?;
    let mut edits = vec![(
        offset(line, name.site.line, name.site.col),
        offset(line, name.site.line, name.site.col) + from.len(),
        to.to_owned(),
    )];
    if let Some(at) = tokens.iter().enumerate().skip(2).find_map(|(i,t)| (t.value=="renamed").then_some(i)) {
        let last = tokens
            .get(at + 2)
            .filter(|_| tokens.get(at + 1).is_some_and(|t| t.value == "from"))
            .ok_or("invalid table origin span")?;
        edits.push((
            offset(line, tokens[at].site.line, tokens[at].site.col),
            offset(line, last.site.line, last.site.col) + last.value.len(),
            prior
                .map(|p| format!("renamed from {p}"))
                .unwrap_or_default(),
        ));
    } else if let Some(prior) = prior {
        let colon = tokens
            .iter()
            .find(|t| t.value == ":")
            .ok_or("missing table header colon")?;
        let a = offset(line, colon.site.line, colon.site.col);
        edits.push((a, a, format!(" renamed from {prior}")));
    }
    let mut out = source.to_owned();
    edits.sort_by_key(|(a, _, _)| std::cmp::Reverse(*a));
    for (a, b, value) in edits {
        out.replace_range(a..b, &value);
    }
    Ok(out)
}
pub(super) fn arg_type(
    text: &str,
    tokens: &[lexer::Token],
    arg: &ast::ArgDecl,
) -> Result<(usize, usize), String> {
    let at = tokens
        .iter()
        .position(|t| t.site == arg.site)
        .ok_or("missing argument source span")?;
    let colon = (at..tokens.len())
        .find(|i| tokens[*i].value == ":")
        .ok_or("missing argument type colon")?;
    let start = tokens.get(colon + 1).ok_or("missing argument type")?;
    let mut depth = 0_i32;
    for (ordinal, t) in tokens.iter().skip(colon + 1).enumerate() {
        // `alias` and `default` are legal first nominal type tokens too.
        if ordinal > 0
            && depth == 0
            && (matches!(
                t.value.as_str(),
                "," | ")" | "=" | "default" | "alias" | "renamed" | "deprecated"
            ) || t.kind == Kind::Newline)
        {
            return Ok((
                offset(text, start.site.line, start.site.col),
                text[..offset(text, t.site.line, t.site.col)]
                    .trim_end()
                    .len(),
            ));
        }
        match t.value.as_str() {
            "(" | "[" | "<" => depth += 1,
            ")" | "]" | ">" => depth -= 1,
            _ => (),
        }
    }
    Err("unterminated argument type source span".into())
}

#[test]
fn scalar_key_rename_retains_an_explicit_legacy_lookup_on_default_rollback() {
    let app=App::new();
    let old="table items:\n  code: text <=20\n  n: int\n  unique byCode: code\n  public\n  online only\nquery legacyLookup(code: text <=20):\n  return items[code] by byCode\n";
    app.source(old);app.ok(&["deploy","deployment"]);
    app.seed(old,vec![json!({"id":"kept","n":1,"code":"a"})]);
    let current=old.replace("  code: text <=20", "  label: text <=20 renamed from code")
        .replace("unique byCode: code","unique byCurrentCode: label").replace("n: int", "n: int ?")
        .replace("query legacyLookup(code: text <=20):\n  return items[code] by byCode\n", "");
    app.source(&current);app.ok(&["deploy","deployment","--check"]);app.ok(&["deploy","deployment"]);
    let preview=app.json(&["rollback","deployment","--to","1","--check","--json"]);
    assert_eq!(preview["visibility"]["hiddenRows"],0);
    app.ok(&["rollback","deployment","--to","1"]);assert_eq!(app.count(),1);
    assert_eq!(app.json(&["sql","SELECT code FROM items","--release","deployment","--json"])["rows"][0][0],"a");
    app.ok(&["rollback","deployment","--to","latest"]);
    assert_eq!(app.json(&["sql","SELECT label FROM items","--release","deployment","--json"])["rows"][0][0],"a");
}

#[test]
fn scalar_renamed_unique_keys_keep_their_identity_in_default_rollback() {
    for (kind, value) in [("text <=20", json!("alice")), ("int", json!(7))] {
        for strengthen in [false,true] {
        let app=App::new();
        let old=format!("table items:\n  n: int\n  code: {kind}\n  memo: text <=20 ?\n  by byN: n, id\n  public\n  online only\ntable retired:\n  value: int\n  public\n  online only\nquery ordered():\n  return items first 10 by byN\n");
        let old=if strengthen {old.replace("  by byN: n, id", "  by byN: n, id\n  by byCustomer: code")} else {old};
        app.source(&old);app.ok(&["deploy","deployment"]);
        app.seed(&old,vec![json!({"id":"kept","n":1,"code":value,"memo":null})]);
        let next=format!("table items:\n  n: int ?\n  label: {kind} renamed from code\n  count: int default 7\n  by byCount: count, label, id\n  unique byCustomer: label\n  public\n  online only\nquery ordered():\n  return items where .count = 7 first 10 by byCount\n");
        app.source(&next);
        app.ok(&["deploy","deployment","--check"]);app.ok(&["deploy","deployment"]);
        let preview=app.json(&["rollback","deployment","--to","1","--check","--json"]);
        assert_eq!(preview["visibility"]["hiddenRows"],0);
        app.ok(&["rollback","deployment","--to","1"]);
        let data=app.json(&["sql","SELECT code FROM items","--release","deployment","--json"]);
        assert_eq!(data["rows"][0][0],value);
        assert_eq!(app.count(),1);
        app.ok(&["rollback","deployment","--to","latest"]);
        let forward=app.json(&["sql","SELECT label FROM items","--release","deployment","--json"]);
        assert_eq!(forward["rows"][0][0],value);
        }
    }
}

impl App {
    fn new() -> Self {
        static NEXT: AtomicU64 = AtomicU64::new(0);
        let p = std::env::temp_dir().join(format!(
            "sb4-roll-{}-{}",
            std::process::id(),
            NEXT.fetch_add(1, Ordering::Relaxed)
        ));
        fs::create_dir_all(p.join("snapback")).unwrap();
        Self(p)
    }
    fn source(&self, s: &str) {
        fs::write(self.0.join("snapback/schema.q"), s).unwrap();
    }
    fn run(&self, args: &[&str]) -> Output {
        Command::new(BIN)
            .args(args)
            .current_dir(&self.0)
            .env_remove("PORT_LO")
            .env_remove("PORT_HI")
            .output()
            .unwrap()
    }
    fn ok(&self, args: &[&str]) -> Output {
        let o = self.run(args);
        assert!(o.status.success(), "{}", text(&o));
        o
    }
    fn json(&self, args: &[&str]) -> Value {
        let o = self.ok(args);
        serde_json::from_slice(&o.stdout).unwrap_or_else(|e| panic!("{e}: {}", text(&o)))
    }
    fn seed(&self, source: &str, rows: Vec<Value>) {
        let b = snapback4_lang::compile(&[("schema.q".into(), source.into())]);
        assert!(b.diagnostics.is_empty(), "{:?}", b.diagnostics);
        let store =
            snapback4_core::store_port::AnyStore::open(&self.0.join("deployment/store.sqlite"))
                .unwrap();
        let n = store.release_value().unwrap().unwrap().parse().unwrap();
        let schema = b.schema.clone();
        let mut e = snapback4_core::Engine::new_release(
            store,
            b.schema,
            b.programs.into_iter().map(|p| p.program),
            n,
            Some(n),
        )
        .unwrap();
        let rows=rows.into_iter().map(|mut row| {
            if row.get("photos")==Some(&json!("fixture-image")) {
                let png=b"\x89PNG\r\n\x1a\n\x00\x00\x00\x0dIHDR\x00\x00\x00\x01\x00\x00\x00\x01";
                let asset=e.store_asset("alice",png,1000).unwrap();
                row["photos"]=json!([snapback4_core::wire::to_json(&asset.column_value())]);
            }
            (
                "items".into(),
                snapback4_core::wire::row_from_json(&schema.tables["items"], &row).unwrap(),
            )
        }).collect::<Vec<_>>();
        e.seed(rows)
        .unwrap();
    }
    fn count(&self) -> u64 {
        self.json(&[
            "sql",
            "SELECT COUNT(*) FROM items",
            "--release",
            "deployment",
            "--json",
        ])["rows"][0][0]
            .as_u64()
            .unwrap()
    }
}
impl Drop for App {
    fn drop(&mut self) {
        let _ = fs::remove_dir_all(&self.0);
    }
}

    format!(
        "{}\n{}",
        String::from_utf8_lossy(&o.stdout),
        String::from_utf8_lossy(&o.stderr)
    )
}
