#[test]
fn default_rollback_uses_a_compiled_guarded_view_and_exact_remains_explicit() {
    let app = App::new();
    app.source(OLD);
    app.ok(&["deploy", "deployment", "--json"]);
    let original = fs::read(app.0.join("deployment/releases/1/snapback/schema.q")).unwrap();
    let next = newer();
    app.source(&next);
    let preview = app.json(&["deploy", "deployment", "--check", "--json"]);
    assert_eq!(preview["rollbackViews"].as_array().unwrap().len(), 1);
    assert_eq!(
        preview["rollbackViewEffects"][0]["visibility"]["hiddenRows"],
        0
    );
    assert_eq!(
        fs::read_dir(app.0.join("deployment/releases"))
            .unwrap()
            .count(),
        1,
        "preview leaves no prepared source"
    );
    let deployed = app.json(&["deploy", "deployment", "--json"]);
    assert_eq!(deployed["release"], 2);
    assert!(deployed["preview"]["clientCompatibility"].is_array());
    app.seed(&next, vec![json!({"id":"pending","amount":null})]);
    for _ in 0..2 {
        let rollback = app.json(&["rollback", "deployment", "--to", "1", "--json"]);
        assert_eq!(rollback["rollbackView"]["target"], 1);
        assert!(rollback["release"].as_u64().unwrap() > 2);
        assert_eq!(app.count(), 1);
        let selected = rollback["release"].as_u64().unwrap();
        let bridge = fs::read_to_string(
            app.0
                .join(format!("deployment/releases/{selected}/snapback/schema.q")),
        )
        .unwrap();
        assert!(
            bridge.contains("require (a.amount != null)")
                || bridge.contains("require a.amount != null"),
            "{bridge}"
        );
        assert!(!bridge.contains("?? 0"));
        app.ok(&["rollback", "deployment", "--to", "latest", "--json"]);
        assert_eq!(app.count(), 1);
    }
    let exact = app.json(&[
        "rollback",
        "deployment",
        "--to",
        "1",
        "--exact",
        "--check",
        "--json",
    ]);
    assert_eq!(exact["visibility"]["hiddenRows"], 1);
    app.ok(&["rollback", "deployment", "--to", "1", "--exact", "--json"]);
    assert_eq!(app.count(), 0);
    app.ok(&["rollback", "deployment", "--to", "latest", "--json"]);
    assert_eq!(app.count(), 1);
    assert_eq!(
        fs::read(app.0.join("deployment/releases/1/snapback/schema.q")).unwrap(),
        original
    );
    let releases = app.json(&["releases", "deployment"]);
    assert_eq!(releases["latest"], 2);
    assert_eq!(releases["rollbackViews"][0]["target"], 1);
    let unchanged = app.json(&["deploy", "deployment", "--json"]);
    assert_eq!(unchanged["unchanged"], true);
}

    app.source(old);
    app.ok(&["deploy", "deployment", "--json"]);

    app.source(next);
    app.ok(&["deploy", "deployment", "--json"]);
    app.seed(
        next,

            json!({"id":"quoted","amount":200}),

        "--release",
        "deployment",
        "--json",
    ]);
    assert_eq!(values["rows"][0][0], 200);
    app.ok(&["rollback", "deployment", "--to", "latest", "--json"]);
    assert_eq!(app.count(), 2);
}

#[test]
fn deploy_verdict_leads_scoped_security_is_last_and_json_keeps_all_notes() {
    let app = App::new();
    let mut old = OLD.to_owned();
    for n in 0..8 {
        old.push_str(&format!(
            "table unchanged{n}:\n  read <- allow\n  online only\n"
        ));
    }
    fs::create_dir_all(app.0.join("versions/v1")).unwrap();
    fs::write(app.0.join("versions/v1/schema.q"), &old).unwrap();
    fs::write(
        app.0.join("snapback/client-compatibility.json"),
        r#"{"baseline":"versions/v1","since_release":2}"#,
    )
    .unwrap();
    app.source(&old);
    app.ok(&["deploy", "deployment", "--json"]);
    let next = old
        .replace("amount: int 100..50000", "amount: int 100..50000 ?")
        .replace(
            "  return insert payments",
            "  require a.amount != null else NO_AMOUNT\n  return insert payments",
        );
    app.source(&next);
    let out = app.ok(&["deploy", "deployment", "--schema", "snapback", "--check"]);
    let stdout = String::from_utf8_lossy(&out.stdout);
    let stderr = String::from_utf8_lossy(&out.stderr);
    assert!(stdout.starts_with("deploy verdict: READY"), "{stdout}");
    assert!(
        stderr
            .lines()
            .last()
            .unwrap()
            .starts_with("deploy verdict: READY"),
        "{stderr}"
    );
    let full = app.json(&[
        "deploy",
        "deployment",
        "--schema",
        "snapback",
        "--check",
        "--json",
    ]);
    assert!(
        full["security"]["report"]["notes"]
            .as_array()
            .unwrap()
            .iter()
            .any(|n| n["message"].as_str().unwrap().contains("unchanged0"))
    );
    assert!(
        !full["security"]["notice"]
            .as_str()
            .unwrap()
            .contains("unchanged0"),
        "{}",
        full["security"]["notice"]
    );
    assert!(full["clientCompatibility"].as_array().unwrap().len() > 1);
}

#[test]
fn failed_guard_transport_does_not_register_or_leave_prepared_views(){
    let app=App::new();let old=OLD.replace("mutation pay", "fn permitted():\n  return viewer = 'alice'\nmutation pay").replace("  a = items[id]", "  require permitted() else FORBIDDEN\n  a = items[id]");
    app.source(&old);app.ok(&["deploy","deployment","--json"]);
    let next=old.replace("amount: int 100..50000", "amount: int 100..50000 ?").replace("viewer = 'alice'", "viewer = 'bob'").replace("  return insert payments", "  require a.amount != null else NO_AMOUNT\n  return insert payments");let next=next.replace("pay(id: items)","pay(item: items)").replace("  a = items[id]","  a = items[item]");app.source(&next);
    for check in [true,false]{let args=if check{vec!["deploy","deployment","--check","--json"]}else{vec!["deploy","deployment","--json"]};let out=app.run(&args);assert!(!out.status.success(),"{}",text(&out));assert!(text(&out).contains("guard dependency"));assert_eq!(fs::read_dir(app.0.join("deployment/releases")).unwrap().count(),1);assert_eq!(app.json(&["releases","deployment"])["current"],1);}
}

#[test]
fn text_minimum_widening_registers_a_view_that_keeps_the_current_quota() {
    let app=App::new();
    let old="table items:\n  owner: principal\n  body: text 4..100\n  cap 2 by owner\n  public 'fixture'\n  insert <- allow\n  online only\nmutation add():\n  return insert items {owner:viewer,body:'kept'}\nquery all():\n  return items first 10 by .id\n";
    app.source(old);app.ok(&["deploy","deployment","--json"]);
    let next=old.replace("text 4..100","text <=100").replace("public 'fixture'","read <- .owner = viewer").replace("cap 2 by owner","cap 1 by owner\n  immutable owner");
    app.source(&next);let preview=app.json(&["deploy","deployment","--check","--json"]);
    assert_eq!(preview["rollbackViews"].as_array().unwrap().len(),1);
    app.ok(&["deploy","deployment","--json"]);app.seed(&next,vec![json!({"id":"short","owner":"alice","body":"x"})]);
    let selected=app.json(&["rollback","deployment","--to","1","--json"])["release"].as_u64().unwrap();assert_eq!(app.count(),1);
    let source=fs::read_to_string(app.0.join(format!("deployment/releases/{selected}/snapback/schema.q"))).unwrap();
    let b=snapback4_lang::compile(&[("schema.q".into(),source)]);assert!(b.diagnostics.is_empty());
    let store=snapback4_core::store_port::AnyStore::open(&app.0.join("deployment/store.sqlite")).unwrap();
    let mut engine=snapback4_core::Engine::new_release(store,b.schema,b.programs.into_iter().map(|p|p.program),selected,Some(selected)).unwrap();
    let hidden=engine.query("all",snapback4_core::Context::new("bob",Default::default())).unwrap();assert_eq!(snapback4_core::wire::to_json(&hidden.data),json!([]));
    let write=engine.mutate("add",None,snapback4_core::Context::new("alice",Default::default())).unwrap();
    assert!(matches!(write.state,snapback4_core::WriteState::Failed{..}),"old quota would have admitted this second row: {write:?}");
    assert_eq!(engine.store().count("items").unwrap(),1);
}

#[test]
fn rollback_uses_active_source_context_after_a_later_branch_changed_privacy() {
    let app=App::new();let old=OLD.replacen("  amount: int 100..50000","  owner: principal\n  amount: int 100..50000",1);
    app.source(&old);app.ok(&["deploy","deployment","--json"]);
    let private=old.replace("amount: int 100..50000","amount: int 100..50000 ?").replacen("  public 'fixture'","  read <- .owner = viewer",1).replace("  return insert payments","  require a.amount != null else NO_AMOUNT\n  return insert payments");
    app.source(&private);let parent=app.json(&["deploy","deployment","--json"])["release"].as_u64().unwrap();
    app.seed(&private,vec![json!({"id":"pending","owner":"alice","amount":null})]);
    let public=private.replacen("  read <- .owner = viewer","  public 'fixture'",1);app.source(&public);app.ok(&["deploy","deployment","--json"]);
    app.ok(&["rollback","deployment","--to",&parent.to_string(),"--exact","--json"]);
    let selected=app.json(&["rollback","deployment","--to","1","--json"]);assert_eq!(selected["rollbackView"]["parent"],parent);
    let release=selected["release"].as_u64().unwrap();let source=fs::read_to_string(app.0.join(format!("deployment/releases/{release}/snapback/schema.q"))).unwrap();
    let b=snapback4_lang::compile(&[("schema.q".into(),source)]);assert!(b.diagnostics.is_empty());let store=snapback4_core::store_port::AnyStore::open(&app.0.join("deployment/store.sqlite")).unwrap();
    let mut e=snapback4_core::Engine::new_release(store,b.schema,b.programs.into_iter().map(|p|p.program),release,Some(release)).unwrap();
    let outcome=e.query("read",snapback4_core::Context::new("bob",std::collections::BTreeMap::from([("id".into(),snapback4_core::Value::Id("pending".into()))]))).unwrap();
    assert_eq!(snapback4_core::wire::to_json(&outcome.data),json!(null));
}


#[test]
fn invariant_apps_keep_ordinary_rename_split_and_required_field_upgrades() {
    // Small independent shapes from wave-25 002/004/020/024/036/044.
    // Real row images, an uploaded image and default rollback exercise storage,
    // rather than relying on a successful check of empty source alone.
    let base="table items:\n  owner: principal\n  immutable owner\n  label: text 1..100\n  read <- .owner = viewer\n  insert <- .owner = viewer\n  online only\nquery all():\n  return items first 10 by .id\nmutation create(label: text 1..100):\n  return insert items {owner:viewer,label}\ninvariant items.read only .owner = viewer\n";
    let rename=base.replace("  label: text 1..100","  title: text 1..100 renamed from label read <- .owner = viewer")
        .replace("create(label: text 1..100)","create(title: text 1..100 alias label)")
        .replace("{owner:viewer,label}","{owner:viewer,title}")
        +"invariant items.title read only .owner = viewer\n";
    let split=base.replace("  label: text 1..100","  invitation: text 1..100\n  envelope: text 1..100")
        .replace("create(label: text 1..100)","create(invitation: text 1..100, envelope: text 1..100)")
        .replace("{owner:viewer,label}","{owner:viewer,invitation,envelope}")
        +"backfill items invitationFromLabel(label: text 1..100): {invitation:label}\nbackfill items envelopeFromLabel(label: text 1..100): {envelope:label}\ndown items oldLabel(invitation: text 1..100): {label:invitation}\ninvariant items.invitation read only .owner = viewer\n";
    let reference=base.replace("table items:","table records renamed from items:")
        .replace("return items","return records").replace("insert items","insert records")
        .replace("invariant items.","invariant records.")
        +"table attachments:\n  parent: records\n  immutable parent\n  read through .parent\n  online only\n";
    let asset_old=base.replace("  label: text 1..100","  label: text 1..100\n  photos: [image <=1000] 1..4")
        .replace("mutation create(label: text 1..100):\n  return insert items {owner:viewer,label}\n","");
    let asset_split=asset_old.replace("  photos: [image <=1000] 1..4\n","")
        +"table surveys:\n  parent: items\n  photos: [image <=1000] 1..4\n  immutable parent\n  read through .parent\n  online only\nextract surveys from items via parent: {photos:photos}\n";
    let alias=reference.replace("mutation create(label:","mutation openRecord(title:")
        .replace("text 1..100):\n  return insert records {owner:viewer,label}","text 1..100 alias label):\n  return insert records {owner:viewer,label:title}")
        +"alias create = openRecord\n";
    let required=base.replace("  label: text 1..100","  label: text 1..100\n  priority: int 1..9 default 3");
    for(name,old,next,row)in [
        ("column rename",base.to_owned(),rename,json!({"id":"kept","owner":"alice","label":"original"})),
        ("split with truthful down",base.to_owned(),split,json!({"id":"kept","owner":"alice","label":"original"})),
        ("nominal rename and child reference",base.to_owned(),reference,json!({"id":"kept","owner":"alice","label":"original"})),
        ("required image to child",asset_old,asset_split,json!({"id":"kept","owner":"alice","label":"original","photos":"fixture-image"})),
        ("table operation and parameter aliases",base.to_owned(),alias,json!({"id":"kept","owner":"alice","label":"original"})),
        ("add required with authored default",base.to_owned(),required,json!({"id":"kept","owner":"alice","label":"original"})),
    ] {
        let app=App::new();app.source(&old);app.ok(&["deploy","deployment"]);app.seed(&old,vec![row]);
        app.source(&next);
        let deployed=app.run(&["deploy","deployment","--json"]);
        assert!(deployed.status.success(),"{name}: {}",text(&deployed));
        let preview=app.json(&["rollback","deployment","--to","1","--check","--json"]);
        assert_eq!(preview["visibility"]["hiddenRows"],0,"{name}: {preview}");
        let rollback=app.run(&["rollback","deployment","--to","1"]);
        assert!(rollback.status.success(),"{name}: {}",text(&rollback));
        assert_eq!(app.count(),1,"{name}");
        let rows=app.json(&["sql","SELECT label FROM items","--release","deployment","--json"]);
        assert_eq!(rows["rows"][0][0],"original","{name}: {rows}");
        app.ok(&["rollback","deployment","--to","latest"]);
    }
}

#[test]
fn pre_view_release_refusal_names_exact_selection_and_registration_upgrade() {
    let app=App::new();app.source(OLD);app.ok(&["deploy","deployment"]);
    let next=newer();app.source(&next);app.ok(&["deploy","deployment"]);
    app.seed(&next,vec![json!({"id":"pending","amount":null})]);
    // A pre-0.4.39 parent has no registration manifest. Its immutable original
    // source and SQLite history are otherwise the same release contract.
    fs::remove_file(app.0.join("deployment/releases/2/rollback-views.json")).unwrap();
    for check in [true,false] {
        let args=if check{vec!["rollback","deployment","--to","1","--check"]}else{vec!["rollback","deployment","--to","1"]};
        let refused=app.run(&args);assert!(!refused.status.success(),"{}",text(&refused));
        assert!(text(&refused).contains("--to 1 --exact"),"{}",text(&refused));
        assert!(text(&refused).contains("redeploy with 0.4.39 to register a view"),"{}",text(&refused));
        assert_eq!(app.count(),1,"refusal must retain the active population");
    }
    app.ok(&["deploy","deployment"]);
    app.ok(&["rollback","deployment","--to","1"]);assert_eq!(app.count(),1);
}

#[test]
fn incomplete_current_shape_uses_a_guarded_bridge_with_current_intent() {
    let app=App::new();
    let old=OLD.replace("  amount: int 100..50000","  legacy: text <=20 ?\n  amount: int 100..50000")+"invariant items.read only true\n";
    app.source(&old);app.ok(&["deploy","deployment"]);
    let next=old.replace("  legacy: text <=20 ?\n","").replace("amount: int 100..50000","amount: int 100..50000 ?")
        .replace("  return insert payments","  require a.amount != null else NO_AMOUNT\n  return insert payments");
    app.source(&next);let preview=app.json(&["deploy","deployment","--check","--json"]);
    assert_eq!(preview["rollbackViews"].as_array().unwrap().len(),1);
    app.ok(&["deploy","deployment"]);
    app.seed(&next,vec![json!({"id":"pending","amount":null})]);
    let selected=app.json(&["rollback","deployment","--to","1","--json"])["release"].as_u64().unwrap();
    assert_eq!(app.count(),1);
    let root=app.0.join(format!("deployment/releases/{selected}/snapback"));
    let sources=fs::read_dir(root).unwrap().map(|entry|{let p=entry.unwrap().path();(p.file_name().unwrap().to_str().unwrap().to_owned(),fs::read_to_string(p).unwrap())}).collect::<Vec<_>>();
    let compiled=snapback4_lang::compile(&sources);assert!(compiled.diagnostics.is_empty(),"{:?}",compiled.diagnostics);
    assert_eq!(compiled.invariants.len(),1);
    assert!(sources.iter().any(|(_,s)|s.contains("NO_AMOUNT")));
    let store=snapback4_core::store_port::AnyStore::open(&app.0.join("deployment/store.sqlite")).unwrap();
    let mut engine=snapback4_core::Engine::new_release(store,compiled.schema,compiled.programs.into_iter().map(|p|p.program),selected,Some(selected)).unwrap();
    let out=engine.mutate("pay",None,snapback4_core::Context::new("alice",std::collections::BTreeMap::from([("id".into(),snapback4_core::Value::Id("pending".into()))]))).unwrap();
    assert!(matches!(out.state,snapback4_core::WriteState::Failed{..}),"{out:?}");
    assert_eq!(engine.store().count("payments").unwrap(),0);
}


#[test]
fn ordinary_quota_tightening_with_an_unchanged_worker_needs_no_view() {
    let app=App::new();
    let old="table items:\n  owner: principal\n  amount: int\n  cap 2 by owner\n  public 'fixture'\n  online only\ninvariant items.read only true\n";
    app.source(old);fs::write(app.0.join("snapback/worker.mjs"),"export {};\n").unwrap();
    app.ok(&["deploy","deployment"]);app.seed(old,vec![json!({"id":"kept","owner":"alice","amount":1})]);
    app.source(&old.replace("cap 2 by owner","cap 1 by owner"));
    let plan=app.json(&["deploy","deployment","--check","--json"]);
    assert_eq!(plan["rollbackViews"].as_array().unwrap().len(),0);
    app.ok(&["deploy","deployment"]);app.ok(&["rollback","deployment","--to","1"]);assert_eq!(app.count(),1);
}


#[test]
fn a_required_column_rename_does_not_block_an_unrelated_widening_view() {
    let app=App::new();let old=OLD.replace("  amount: int 100..50000","  state: enum('old') default 'old'\n  amount: int 100..50000")+"invariant items.read only true\n";
    app.source(&old);app.ok(&["deploy","deployment"]);
    let next=old.replace("state: enum('old')","state: enum('old','new')")
        .replace("  amount: int 100..50000","  quotedAmount: int 100..50000 renamed from amount")
        .replace("amount:a.amount","amount:a.quotedAmount");
    app.source(&next);app.ok(&["deploy","deployment"]);
    app.seed(&next,vec![json!({"id":"kept","state":"new","quotedAmount":123})]);
    let preview=app.json(&["rollback","deployment","--to","1","--check","--json"]);assert_eq!(preview["visibility"]["hiddenRows"],0);
    app.ok(&["rollback","deployment","--to","1"]);assert_eq!(app.count(),1);
    let data=app.json(&["sql","SELECT quotedAmount FROM items","--release","deployment","--json"]);assert_eq!(data["rows"][0][0],123);
}

#[test]
fn ordinary_constraint_rename_with_unchanged_worker_does_not_require_a_view() {
    let app=App::new();
    let old="table codes:\n  code: text <=20\n  unique code\n  public\n  online only\ntable records:\n  value: text <=20\n  public\n  after insert <- exists codes[code = .value]\n  online only\ninvariant records.read only true\n";
    app.source(old);fs::write(app.0.join("snapback/worker.mjs"),"export {};\n").unwrap();
    app.ok(&["deploy","deployment"]);
    let next=old.replace("table codes:","table catalog renamed from codes:")
        .replace("exists codes[","exists catalog[");
    app.source(&next);
    let plan=app.json(&["deploy","deployment","--check","--json"]);
    assert_eq!(plan["rollbackViews"].as_array().unwrap().len(),0);
    app.ok(&["deploy","deployment"]);
    let plan=app.json(&["rollback","deployment","--to","1","--check","--json"]);
    assert_eq!(plan["visibility"]["hiddenRows"],0);
    app.ok(&["rollback","deployment","--to","1"]);
}

new file mode 100644

+//! Compare aligned policy meaning without confusing a key with its label.
+// @ref LLP 3000.000.000#20-visible-rollback-views-roll — compiled key meaning and every guard operand survive a visible rollback.
+use snapback4_core::{Schema, schema::{Constraint, Horizon, RuleExpr, Sync}};
+
+pub struct Policies<'a> {
+    view: &'a Schema,
+    current: &'a Schema,
+}
+
+impl<'a> Policies<'a> {
+    pub fn new(view: &'a Schema, current: &'a Schema) -> Self { Self { view, current } }
+
+    fn index(&self, table: &str, view: &str, current: &str) -> bool {
+        match (self.view.table(table).and_then(|t|t.indexes.get(view)),
+            self.current.table(table).and_then(|t|t.indexes.get(current))) {
+            (Some(view),Some(current))=>view==current,
+            _=>false,
+        }
+    }
+
+    pub fn constraint(&self, view: &Constraint, current: &Constraint) -> bool {
+        match (view,current) {
+            (Constraint::FinalExistsOnInsert{table:a,index:ai,key:ak},
+                Constraint::FinalExistsOnInsert{table:b,index:bi,key:bk})=>
+                a==b&&ak==bk&&self.index(a,ai,bi),
+            _=>view==current,
+        }
+    }
+
+    pub fn rule(&self, view: &RuleExpr, current: &RuleExpr) -> bool {
+        use RuleExpr::*;
+        match (view,current) {
+            (Exists{table:a,index:ai,key:ak,provenance:ap,equalities:ae},
+                Exists{table:b,index:bi,key:bk,provenance:bp,equalities:be}) |
+            (Created{table:a,index:ai,key:ak,provenance:ap,equalities:ae},
+                Created{table:b,index:bi,key:bk,provenance:bp,equalities:be})=>
+                a==b&&ak==bk&&ap==bp&&ae==be&&self.index(a,ai,bi),
+            (And(a),And(b))|(Or(a),Or(b))=>a.len()==b.len()&&a.iter().zip(b).all(|(a,b)|self.rule(a,b)),
+            (Not(a),Not(b))=>self.rule(a,b),
+            _=>view==current,
+        }
+    }
+
+    pub fn optional_rule(&self, view: Option<&RuleExpr>, current: Option<&RuleExpr>) -> bool {
+        match (view,current) { (Some(a),Some(b))=>self.rule(a,b),(None,None)=>true,_=>false }
+    }
+
+    fn horizon(&self, table: &str, view: Option<&Horizon>, current: Option<&Horizon>) -> bool {
+        match (view,current) {
+            (Some(a),Some(b))=>a.last==b.last&&self.index(table,&a.by,&b.by),
+            (None,None)=>true,
+            _=>false,
+        }
+    }
+
+    pub fn sync(&self, table: &str, view: Option<&Sync>, current: Option<&Sync>) -> bool {
+        match (view,current) {
+            (Some(a),Some(b))=>a.audience==b.audience&&self.horizon(table,a.horizon.as_ref(),b.horizon.as_ref())
+                &&a.also.len()==b.also.len()&&a.also.iter().zip(&b.also).all(|(a,b)|
+                    a.audience==b.audience&&self.horizon(table,a.horizon.as_ref(),b.horizon.as_ref())),
+            (None,None)=>true,
+            _=>false,
+        }
+    }
+}
+
+#[cfg(test)]
+mod tests {
+    use super::*;
+    use snapback4_core::{Value, ir::KeyProvenance, schema::{Audience, RuleTerm}};
+    fn schema(name: &str, key: &str) -> Schema {
+        let source=format!("table target:\n  code: text <=20\n  other: text <=20\n  unique {name}: {key}\n  public\n  online only\n");
+        let bound=crate::compile(&[("schema.q".into(),source)]);
+        assert!(bound.diagnostics.is_empty(),"{:?}",bound.diagnostics);bound.schema
+    }
+    fn probe(name: &str) -> RuleExpr {
+        RuleExpr::Exists{table:"target".into(),index:name.into(),key:vec![RuleTerm::Field("value".into())],provenance:KeyProvenance::Shape,equalities:Vec::new()}
+    }
+    #[test]
+    fn equivalent_labels_keep_all_probe_operands_and_provenance() {
+        let view=schema("oldKey","code");let current=schema("newKey","code");let policy=Policies::new(&view,&current);
+        let a=probe("oldKey");let b=probe("newKey");assert!(policy.rule(&a,&b));
+        let mut changed=b.clone();if let RuleExpr::Exists{key,..}=&mut changed{*key=vec![RuleTerm::Field("different".into())];}assert!(!policy.rule(&a,&changed));

pub fn int_bound(end: i64, limit: i64) -> Option<i64> { (end != limit).then_some(end) }

/// Whether `value` lies inside an inclusive optional range.
pub fn int_in_range(value: i64, min: Option<i64>, max: Option<i64>) -> bool {
    min.is_none_or(|min| value >= min) && max.is_none_or(|max| value <= max)
}

#[derive(Clone, Debug, PartialEq, Eq, Serialize, Deserialize)]
pub struct Index {
    pub components: Vec<IndexComponent>,
    pub unique: bool,
}

impl Index {
    pub fn of(columns: &[&str], unique: bool) -> Self {
        Self { components: columns.iter().map(|c| IndexComponent::Column((*c).to_owned())).collect(), unique }
    }

    /// Ordered key names: columns and time-function names. A `words(..)`
    /// component contributes nothing here; `word_component` names it.
    /// Use `IndexComponent::column` for actual row fields (such as rule pins).
    pub fn columns(&self) -> Vec<String> {
        self.components.iter().filter_map(|c| c.key_name().cloned()).collect()
    }

    pub fn is_plain(&self) -> bool {
        self.components.iter().all(|c| matches!(c, IndexComponent::Column(_)))
    }

    pub fn uses_column(&self, name: &str) -> bool {
        self.components.iter().any(|component| match component {
            IndexComponent::Column(column) => column == name,
            IndexComponent::Calendar(key) => key.column == name,
            IndexComponent::WordPrefixes { fields, .. } => fields.iter().any(|field| field == name),
            IndexComponent::Contribution(maintain) => maintain.dependencies().contains(name),
        })
    }

    /// The `words(..)` component, if any: its position among the
    /// components and the text columns it tokenizes.
    pub fn word_component(&self) -> Option<(usize, &[String])> {
        self.components.iter().enumerate().find_map(|(position, component)| match component {
            IndexComponent::WordPrefixes { fields, .. } => Some((position, fields.as_slice())),
            IndexComponent::Column(_) | IndexComponent::Calendar(_) | IndexComponent::Contribution(_) => None,
        })
    }

    /// The columns after the first `components` components: a cursor's
    /// suffix, and the order a page continues in. Counted in components,
    /// so a scan whose prefix ends with a word skips the word too.
    pub fn suffix_after(&self, components: usize) -> Vec<String> {
        self.components.iter().skip(components).filter_map(|c| c.key_name().cloned()).collect()
    }
}

#[derive(Clone, Debug, PartialEq, Eq, Serialize, Deserialize)]
pub enum IndexComponent {
    /// Internal selected physical source rows; an optional following expiry component
    /// orders the group by deadline (null last), then the appended source id.
    Contribution(Box<Maintain>),
    Column(String),
    Calendar(crate::maintain::CalendarKey),
    WordPrefixes { fields: Vec<String>, #[serde(default)] contract: serde_json::Value },
}

impl IndexComponent {
    pub fn key_name(&self) -> Option<&String> {
        match self { Self::Calendar(key) => Some(&key.function), _ => self.column() }
    }
    pub fn key_type(&self, table: &Table) -> Option<ColumnType> {
        match self { Self::Calendar(_) => Some(ColumnType::Int), Self::Column(name) => table.columns.get(name).cloned(), _ => None }
    }
    pub fn value(&self, row: &Value) -> Option<Value> {
        match self { Self::Calendar(key) => Some(key.value(row)), Self::Column(name) => Some(row.field(name).cloned().unwrap_or(Value::Null)), _ => None }
    }
    pub fn column(&self) -> Option<&String> {
        match self {
            Self::Column(column) => Some(column),
            Self::Calendar(_) | Self::WordPrefixes { .. } | Self::Contribution(_) => None,
        }
    }
}

#[derive(Clone, Debug, PartialEq, Eq, Serialize, Deserialize)]
pub struct Rules {
    pub read: RuleExpr,
    pub insert: RuleExpr,
    pub update: RuleExpr,
    pub delete: RuleExpr,
}

impl Rules {
    pub fn deny() -> Self {
        Self { read: RuleExpr::Deny, insert: RuleExpr::Deny, update: RuleExpr::Deny, delete: RuleExpr::Deny }
    }

    pub fn for_effect(&self, effect: Effect) -> &RuleExpr {
        match effect {
            Effect::Read => &self.read,
            Effect::Insert => &self.insert,
            Effect::Update => &self.update,
            Effect::Delete => &self.delete,
        }
    }
}

#[derive(Clone, Copy, Debug, PartialEq, Eq, Serialize, Deserialize)]
pub enum Effect {
    Read,
    Insert,
    Update,
    Delete,
}

impl fmt::Display for Effect {
    fn fmt(&self, f: &mut fmt::Formatter<'_>) -> fmt::Result {
        f.write_str(match self {
            Self::Read => "read",
            Self::Insert => "insert",
            Self::Update => "update",
            Self::Delete => "delete",
        })
    }
}

#[derive(Clone, Debug, PartialEq, Eq, Serialize, Deserialize)]
pub enum RuleExpr {
    Public { reason: String },
    Allow,
    Deny,
    Eq(RuleTerm, RuleTerm),
    Ne(RuleTerm, RuleTerm),
    /// Ordered comparisons are confined to named update transitions and to
    /// insert rules over the row's own required int/time columns.
    Compare(crate::ir::BinaryOp, RuleTerm, RuleTerm),
    And(Vec<RuleExpr>),
    Or(Vec<RuleExpr>),
    Not(Box<RuleExpr>),
    Exists {
        table: String, index: String, key: Vec<RuleTerm>, provenance: KeyProvenance,
        #[serde(default, skip_serializing_if = "Vec::is_empty")]
        equalities: Vec<(String, RuleTerm)>,
    },
    /// True only when the indexed target row was inserted earlier in this
    /// same transaction; insert rules only.
    Created {

"""EV-1 public release witness, using the serving harness's owned cluster/children."""
# @ref LLP 3000.009.001.000#3.5 — configured deploy/serve and a store-independent wire comparison.
import json, time

V1 = '''use identity
table notes:
  n: int
  memo: json <=100 ?
  site: text <=200 format url ?
  by byN: n, id
  public
  insert <- allow
  update <- allow
  online only
table retired:
  text: text <=100
  public
  insert <- allow
  online only
mutation put(key: id, n: int, memo: json <=100 ?, site: text <=200 format url ?):
  upsert notes[key] {n, memo, site}
  return n
mutation archive(key: id, text: text <=100):
  insert retired {id: key, text}
  return text
query peek(key: id):
  return notes[key]
query archived(key: id):
  return retired[key]
query ordered():
  return notes first 10 by byN
'''
V2 = '''use identity
table notes:
  n: int
  extra: text <=100 ?
  count: int default 7
  by byCount: count, id
  public
  insert <- allow
  update <- allow
  online only
table added:
  n: int
  public
  insert <- allow
  online only
mutation put(key: id, n: int):
  upsert notes[key] {n}
  return n
mutation add(key: id, n: int):
  insert added {id: key, n}
  return n
query peek(key: id):
  return notes[key]
query addedRow(key: id):
  return added[key]
query ordered():
  return notes where .count = 7 first 10 by byCount
'''

V3 = V1.replace('  memo: json <=100 ?\n', '  memo: json <=100 ?\n  extra: text <=100 ?\n  count: int default 7\n')

def witness(h, pg_url):
    work, cli, start, stop, http = (h[k] for k in ('WORK','cli','start','stop','http'))
    transcripts = {}
    for backend, target in [('postgres', pg_url), ('sqlite', None)]:
        deployment = work / ('ev1-' + backend)
        records = []
        assert V3 != V1 and 'format url ?' in V3
        for version, source in [(1,V1),(2,V2),(3,V3)]:
            (work/'app/snapback/schema.q').write_text(source)
            args = ['deploy', str(deployment)]
            if version == 3: args += ['--restore-dormant']
            cli(args, url=target)
            child, base = start('ev1-'+backend+'-v'+str(version),target,deployment=deployment)
            guest = http(base,'POST','/auth/guest',{})['session']
            token = guest['token']
            state = http(base,'GET','/schema',token=token)
            assert state['generation']==version,state
            def capture(label, value):
                # Each backend mints its own guest principal. Check its value
                # before excluding that nondeterministic identity from comparison.
                if ' q ' in label: assert value['viewer']==guest['principal'],value
                if 'store_id' in value: assert value['store_id']==state['store_id'],value
                independent = {k:v for k,v in value.items() if k not in ('store_id','now','viewer')}
                records.append((label,json.dumps(independent,sort_keys=True,separators=(',',':'))))
                return value
            def mutate(name, identity, args):
                return capture(f'v{version} m {name}',http(base,'POST','/m/'+name,{'id':identity,'args':args,'store_id':state['store_id']},token))
            def query(name, args):
                return capture(f'v{version} q {name}',http(base,'POST','/q/'+name,{'args':args},token))
            if version == 1:
                assert mutate('put','ev1-a',{'key':'a','n':2,'memo':'雪\0exact','site':'https://example.com'})['state']=='sent'
                assert mutate('put','ev1-b',{'key':'b','n':1,'memo':None,'site':None})['state']=='sent'
                assert mutate('archive','ev1-z',{'key':'z','text':'retained row'})['state']=='sent'
                first = query('peek',{'key':'a'})['data']
                archived = query('archived',{'key':'z'})['data']
                assert [r['n'] for r in query('ordered',{})['data']]==[1,2]
            elif version == 2:
                changed = query('peek',{'key':'a'})['data']
                assert changed == {'id':'a','n':2,'extra':None,'count':7},changed
                assert len(query('ordered',{})['data'])==2
                assert mutate('add','ev1-new',{'key':'new','n':9})['state']=='sent'
                assert query('addedRow',{'key':'new'})['data']['n']==9
            else:
                assert query('peek',{'key':'a'})['data']==dict(first,extra=None,count=7)
                assert query('archived',{'key':'z'})['data']==archived
                assert [r['n'] for r in query('ordered',{})['data']]==[1,2]
            stop(child)
            if backend == 'postgres':
                deadline=time.monotonic()+15
                while True:
                    doctor=json.loads(cli(['doctor','--release',str(deployment),'--json'],url=target).stdout)
                    if doctor['store']['expired']: break
                    assert time.monotonic()<deadline,doctor
                    time.sleep(.1)
        transcripts[backend]=json.dumps(records,sort_keys=True,separators=(',',':')).encode()
        (work/('ev1-'+backend+'-wire.json')).write_bytes(transcripts[backend])
        print('P1-EV-1',backend,'PASS: v1 writes, v2 additions/retirements/indexes, v3 exact restoration',flush=True)
    assert transcripts['postgres']==transcripts['sqlite'], 'EV-1 wire bytes differ'
    (work/'app/snapback/schema.q').write_text(h['source'])
    print('P1-EV-1 PASS: byte-identical wire after removing store_id, now and the checked minted guest viewer',flush=True)
    large_default_witness(h, pg_url.replace('schema=ev1', 'schema=ev1_large'))


def large_default_witness(h, pg_url):
    """A restored raw parent image must widen /changes identically on both stores."""

    sibling = "  payload: text <=307200 default '" + 'x' * (300 * 1024) + "'\n"

                    assert mutate('erase')['state'] == 'sent'

                    assert time.monotonic() < deadline, doctor

    assert answers['sqlite']['changes'] is None, answers
    assert answers['postgres'] == answers['sqlite'], '300 KiB restored parent /changes differs'
    (work/'ev1-large-changes.json').write_text(json.dumps(answers, sort_keys=True))

     2	// @ref LLP 3000.000.000.002#8-wave-21-release-evolution — generated source is reviewable; no automatic business-policy synthesis.

     5	pub struct Bridge {pub sources:Vec<(String,String)>,pub changes:Vec<String>,pub diagnostics:Vec<crate::diag::Diagnostic>}

    12	/// Source-domain inclusion includes text minima/formats, which live outside
    13	/// ColumnType in the compiled schema. Never infer admission from advice prose.
    14	pub fn requires_view(target:&[(String,String)],current:&[(String,String)])->bool {
    15	    // An executable repair behind unchanged data and intent must not restore
    16	    // the old implementation. Ordinary schema/intent evolution is governed by
    17	    // visibility facts below, rather than the mere presence of an invariant.
    18	    if same_data_and_intent(target,current)
    19	        && !crate::compile(current).invariants.is_empty()
    20	        && modules(target)!=modules(current) {return true;}
    21	    let docs=current.iter().map(|(f,t)|parser::parse_document(f,t).0).collect::<Vec<_>>();
    22	    let tables=docs.iter().flat_map(|d|&d.tables).collect::<Vec<_>>();
    23	    let mut aliases=tables.iter().filter_map(|t|t.renamed_from.as_ref().map(|old|(t.name.clone(),old.clone()))).collect::<BTreeMap<_,_>>();
    24	    for(file,text)in target{for t in parser::parse_document(file,text).0.tables{if let Some(prior)=t.renamed_from{aliases.insert(t.name,prior);}}}
    25	    fn normalize(t:&mut ast::ScalarType,aliases:&BTreeMap<String,String>){match &mut t.kind{TypeKind::Ref{table}=>{let mut seen=std::collections::BTreeSet::new();while let Some(old)=aliases.get(table){if !seen.insert(table.clone()){*table=seen.into_iter().min().unwrap();break}*table=old.clone();}},TypeKind::List{item,..}=>normalize(item,aliases),_=>()}}
    26	    if super::release_bridge_constraints::relaxed(target,current) {return true;}
    27	    for(file,text)in target {
    28	        for old in parser::parse_document(file,text).0.tables {
    29	            let Some(next)=tables.iter().find(|t|t.name==old.name||t.renamed_from.as_ref()==Some(&old.name)||old.renamed_from.as_ref()==Some(&t.name))else{continue};
    30	            for col in &old.columns {
    31	                let Some(candidate)=next.columns.iter().find(|c|c.name==col.name||c.renamed_from.as_ref()==Some(&col.name)||col.renamed_from.as_ref()==Some(&c.name))else{continue};
    32	                let scalar=!matches!(&col.type_.kind,TypeKind::Ref{..}|TypeKind::List{..}|TypeKind::Image{..}|TypeKind::File{..}|TypeKind::Video{..});
    33	                if scalar&&docs.iter().flat_map(|d|&d.downfills).any(|d|d.table==next.name&&matches!(&d.value,ast::Expr::Object{fields,..}if fields.iter().any(|f|f.name==col.name))){continue;}
    34	                let mut from=candidate.type_.clone();let mut to=col.type_.clone();normalize(&mut from,&aliases);normalize(&mut to,&aliases);
    35	                if !crate::client_contracts::accepts(&crate::types::scalar_to_arg(&from),&crate::types::scalar_to_arg(&to)){return true}
    36	            }
    37	            let Some(columns)=declared_column_names(&old,next)else{return true};
    38	            let normalize_columns=|fields:&[String]|fields.iter().map(|c|columns.get(c).unwrap_or(c).clone()).collect::<Vec<_>>();
    39	            if old.indexes.iter().any(|i|i.unique&&!next.indexes.iter().any(|n|n.unique&&normalize_columns(&n.columns)==i.columns)){return true}
    40	            if old.caps.iter().any(|c|!next.caps.iter().any(|n|normalize_columns(&n.by)==c.by&&n.max<=c.max)){return true}
    41	        }
    42	    }false
    43	}

    45	fn modules(sources:&[(String,String)])->Vec<(String,String)> {
    46	    let mut out=sources.iter().map(|(file,text)|(std::path::Path::new(file).file_stem().unwrap_or(std::ffi::OsStr::new("schema")).to_string_lossy().into_owned(),text.clone())).collect::<Vec<_>>();out.sort();out
    47	}

    49	pub(super) fn operation_name(file:&str,name:&str)->String {
    50	    let module=std::path::Path::new(file).file_stem().and_then(|s|s.to_str()).unwrap_or("schema");
    51	    if module=="schema"{name.to_owned()}else{format!("{module}.{name}")}
    52	}

    54	/// Compare authored data/intent declarations and executable signatures, while
    55	/// excluding executable bodies. Module identity and wire signatures still count.
    56	pub fn same_data_and_intent(target:&[(String,String)],current:&[(String,String)])->bool {
    57	    fn signature(sources:&[(String,String)])->Vec<(String,Vec<(lexer::Kind,String)>)> {
    58	        modules(sources).into_iter().map(|(module,text)| {
    59	            let doc=parser::parse_document(&module,&text).0;
    60	            let mut edits=Vec::new();
    61	            for site in doc.programs.iter().map(|p|&p.site).chain(doc.functions.iter().map(|p|&p.site)) {
    62	                let start=offset(&text,site.line,1);
    63	                let declaration=declaration(&text,site.line);
    64	                let (tokens,_)=lexer::lex(&module,&declaration);
    65	                let mut depth=0;
    66	                if let Some(colon)=tokens.iter().find(|token| {
    67	                    match token.value.as_str(){"("|"["|"{"=>depth+=1,")"|"]"|"}"=>depth-=1,_=>()}
    68	                    token.value==":"&&depth==0
    69	                }) {
    70	                    let end=(start+declaration.len()).min(text.len());
    71	                    let body=start+offset(&declaration,colon.site.line,colon.site.col)+1;
    72	                    edits.push((body,end));
    73	                }
    74	            }
    75	            let mut text=text;
    76	            edits.sort_by_key(|e|std::cmp::Reverse(e.0));
    77	            for (start,end) in edits {text.replace_range(start..end,"\n");}
    78	            let (tokens,_)=lexer::lex(&module,&text);
    79	            (module,tokens.into_iter().map(|t|(t.kind,t.value)).collect())
    80	        }).collect()
    81	    }
    82	    signature(target)==signature(current)
    83	}

    85	pub fn loosen(target:&[(String,String)],current:&[(String,String)])->Bridge {
    86	    if !crate::compile(current).invariants.is_empty() {
    87	        let mut allow_guarded=false;
    88	        let complete=super::release_bridge_current::prepare(target,current,&mut allow_guarded);
    89	        if complete.diagnostics.is_empty() || !allow_guarded{return complete;}
    90	        // A complete current closure is preferred, but a selective guarded
    91	        // predecessor is also truthful when its retained contracts compile.
    92	        let guarded=super::release_bridge_current::guarded(target,current);
    93	        if guarded.diagnostics.is_empty(){return guarded;}
    94	        return Bridge{diagnostics:complete.diagnostics.into_iter().chain(guarded.diagnostics).collect(),..guarded};
    95	    }
    96	    let guarded=loosen_guarded(target,current);
    97	    if !guarded.diagnostics.is_empty(){
    98	        let complete=super::release_bridge_current::prepare(target,current,&mut false);
    99	        if complete.diagnostics.is_empty(){return complete;}
   100	        let current_bridge=super::release_bridge_current::guarded(target,current);
   101	        if current_bridge.diagnostics.is_empty(){return current_bridge;}
   102	    }
   103	    guarded
   104	}

   130	                        TypeKind::List{item,..}=>normalize(item,tables),
   131	                        TypeKind::Enum{variants}=>variants.sort(), _=>()
   132	                    }
   133	                }

   135	                if current_domain!=joined_domain {
   136	                    domain_diagnostics.push(crate::diag::type_error(&column.site,format!("rollback view for {}.{} would admit predecessor values excluded by the current domain; current authority may depend on their absence",table.name,column.name),"author a compatible rollback source preserving the current domain and truthful migrations; do not widen a current confidentiality or safety boundary").guide("deploy"));
   137	                }
   138	                if joined==column.type_ {continue;}
   139	                affected.insert(table.name.clone());
   140	                if let Some((start,end))=type_span(file,text,column) {
   141	                    edits.push((start,end,print::scalar_type(&joined)));
   142	                    changes.push(format!("{}.{}: {} → {}",table.name,column.name,print::scalar_type(&column.type_),print::scalar_type(&joined)));
   143	                }
   144	            }
   145	        }
   146	        edits.sort_by_key(|e|std::cmp::Reverse(e.0));for(start,end,value)in edits{text.replace_range(start..end,&value);}

   152	        if current_bridge.diagnostics.is_empty(){return current_bridge;}

   227	            let old_name=column.renamed_from.as_ref().filter(|name|table.columns.contains_key(*name)).unwrap_or(&column.name);
   228	            if table.columns.contains_key(old_name)&&table.defaults.get(old_name)!=current_table.defaults.get(&column.name) {
   229	                diagnostics.push(crate::diag::type_error(&column.site,format!("rollback view cannot preserve current default for {name}.{}",column.name),"retain the current authored default in a compatible guarded rollback source").guide("deploy"));
   230	            }
   231	        }
   232	    }
   233	    if bound.diagnostics.is_empty() && !super::release_bridge_current::contracts(&crate::compile(target),&bound) {
   234	        diagnostics.push(crate::diag::type_error(&crate::Site::default(),"rollback view cannot preserve every predecessor wire contract, including argument aliases and distinct input positions","retain the old parameter aliases on compatible current endpoints, or author a guarded compatibility endpoint before deploying").guide("deploy"));
   235	    }
   236	    diagnostics.extend(bound.diagnostics);diagnostics.extend(domain_diagnostics);
   237	    Bridge {sources,changes,diagnostics}
   238	}
   239	fn offset(text:&str,line:u32,col:u32)->usize {
   240	    let start=text.split_inclusive('\n').take(line.saturating_sub(1) as usize).map(str::len).sum::<usize>();
   241	    start+text[start..].chars().take(col.saturating_sub(1) as usize).map(char::len_utf8).sum::<usize>()
   242	}
   243	pub fn type_span(file:&str,text:&str,column:&ast::ColumnDecl)->Option<(usize,usize)> {
   244	    let(tokens,_)=lexer::lex(file,text);let at=tokens.iter().position(|t|t.site==column.site)?;
   245	    let start=tokens.get(at+2)?;let mut depth=0;
   246	    for (ordinal,token) in tokens.iter().skip(at+2).enumerate() {
   247	        // A nominal type may itself be named `default`, `read`, etc. Only
   248	        // recognize metadata after consuming the first token of the type.
   249	        if ordinal>0 && depth==0 && (token.kind==Kind::Newline || matches!(token.value.as_str(),"default"|"renamed"|"on"|"read"|"written"|"readable")) {
   250	            // End before whitespace preceding metadata/comment/newline.
   251	            let end=offset(text,token.site.line,token.site.col);
   252	            let start=offset(text,start.site.line,start.site.col);let end=text[..end].trim_end().len();
   253	            return (start<=end).then_some((start,end));
   254	        }
   255	        match token.value.as_str(){"("|"["=>depth+=1,")"|"]"=>depth-=1,_=>()}
   256	    }None
   257	}
   258	fn declaration(text:&str,line:u32)->String {
   259	    let lines:Vec<_>=text.lines().collect();let start=line.saturating_sub(1) as usize;let mut end=start+1;
   260	    while end<lines.len() {let s=lines[end];if !s.trim().is_empty() && !s.starts_with(char::is_whitespace) && !s.starts_with("--"){break;}end+=1;}
   261	    lines[start..end].join("\n")+"\n"
   262	}
   263	// Rewrite only structural table references; strings, column names and bound
   264	// rule parameters retain their spelling and policy meaning.
   265	pub(super) fn retained_declaration(file:&str,text:&str,table:&ast::TableDecl,aliases:&BTreeMap<String,String>)->Result<String,String> {
   266	    let mut source=declaration(text,table.site.line);
   267	    let mut decl=parser::parse_document(file,&source).0.tables.into_iter().next().ok_or("missing retained table declaration")?;
   268	    let mut edits=Vec::new();
   269	    fn remap_type(t:&mut ast::ScalarType,aliases:&BTreeMap<String,String>) {
   270	        match &mut t.kind {TypeKind::Ref{table}=>{if let Some(name)=aliases.get(table){*table=name.clone();}},TypeKind::List{item,..}=>remap_type(item,aliases),_=>()}
   271	    }
   272	    for column in &decl.columns {
   273	        let mut type_=column.type_.clone();remap_type(&mut type_,aliases);
   274	        if type_!=column.type_ {if let Some((start,end))=type_span(file,&source,column){edits.push((start,end,print::scalar_type(&type_)));}}
   275	    }
   276	    let mut visit=|expr:&mut ast::Expr| {
   277	        if let ast::Expr::Read(read)|ast::Expr::Exists{read,..}|ast::Expr::Created{read,..}=expr {
   278	            if let Some(name)=aliases.get(&read.table) {let start=offset(&source,read.site.line,read.site.col);edits.push((start,start+read.table.len(),name.clone()));}
   279	        }
   280	    };
   281	    for rule in decl.rules.iter_mut().chain(decl.column_reads.iter_mut().map(|(_,r)|r)) {crate::bind::intent_walk::expression(&mut rule.value,&mut visit);}
   282	    for column in &mut decl.columns {
   283	        for expr in [&mut column.read,&mut column.default].into_iter().flatten(){crate::bind::intent_walk::expression(expr,&mut visit);}
   284	        if let Some(expr)=column.maintained.as_mut().and_then(|a|a.filter.as_mut()){crate::bind::intent_walk::expression(expr,&mut visit);}
   285	    }
   286	    for (expr,_) in &mut decl.final_exists {crate::bind::intent_walk::expression(expr,&mut visit);}
   287	    // Sync targets are structural table probes outside the expression AST.
   288	    if let Some(sync)=&decl.sync {
   289	        let (tokens,_)=lexer::lex(file,&source);
   290	        // Lexer continuation rules keep indented `or` arms in this logical
   291	        // declaration even when their physical line differs from `sync`.
   292	        if let Some(start)=tokens.iter().position(|t|t.site==sync.site) {
   293	            let end=tokens[start..].iter().position(|t|matches!(t.kind,Kind::Newline|Kind::Eof)).map_or(tokens.len(),|n|start+n);
   294	            for pair in tokens[start..end].windows(2) {if pair[0].kind==Kind::Word && pair[1].value=="[" {if let Some(name)=aliases.get(&pair[0].value){let start=offset(&source,pair[0].site.line,pair[0].site.col);edits.push((start,start+pair[0].value.len(),name.clone()));}}}
   295	        }
   296	    }
   297	    edits.sort();edits.dedup();
   298	    if edits.iter().any(|(a,b,_)|a>b||*b>source.len()||!source.is_char_boundary(*a)||!source.is_char_boundary(*b))
   299	        || edits.windows(2).any(|pair|pair[0].1>pair[1].0) {
   300	        return Err("unsupported or overlapping retained-table source spans".into());
   301	    }
   302	    edits.sort_by_key(|e|std::cmp::Reverse(e.0));
   303	    for (start,end,value) in edits {source.replace_range(start..end,&value);}
   304	    Ok(source)
   305	}
   306	fn join(old:&ast::ScalarType,new:&ast::ScalarType,tables:&BTreeMap<String,(&String,&String,ast::TableDecl)>)->Option<ast::ScalarType> {
   307	    let kind=match (&old.kind,&new.kind) {
   308	        (TypeKind::Int|TypeKind::IntRange{..},TypeKind::Int|TypeKind::IntRange{..})=>{
   309	            let bounds=|k:&TypeKind|match k{TypeKind::IntRange{min,max}=>(min.unwrap_or(i64::MIN),max.unwrap_or(i64::MAX)),_=>(i64::MIN,i64::MAX)};
   310	            let(a,b)=bounds(&old.kind);let(x,y)=bounds(&new.kind);let(min,max)=(a.min(x),b.max(y));
   311	            if min==i64::MIN && max==i64::MAX {TypeKind::Int}else{TypeKind::IntRange{min:(min!=i64::MIN).then_some(min),max:(max!=i64::MAX).then_some(max)}}
   312	        }
   313	        (TypeKind::Image{max:a,once:b},TypeKind::Image{max:x,once:y}) if b==y=>TypeKind::Image{max:(*a).max(*x),once:*b},
   314	        (TypeKind::Video{max:a},TypeKind::Video{max:b})=>TypeKind::Video{max:(*a).max(*b)},
   315	        (TypeKind::File{max:a,types:b},TypeKind::File{max:x,types:y}) if b==y=>TypeKind::File{max:(*a).max(*x),types:b.clone()},
   316	        (TypeKind::Text{min:a,max:b,format:c},TypeKind::Text{min:x,max:y,format:z}) if c==z=>TypeKind::Text{min:Some(a.unwrap_or(0).min(x.unwrap_or(0))).filter(|v|*v>0),max:(*b).max(*y),format:c.clone()},
   317	        (TypeKind::Enum{variants:a},TypeKind::Enum{variants:b})=>{let mut v=a.clone();for x in b {if !v.contains(x){v.push(x.clone());}}TypeKind::Enum{variants:v}},
   318	        (TypeKind::List{item:a,min:b,max:c},TypeKind::List{item:x,min:y,max:z})=>TypeKind::List{item:Box::new(join(a,x,tables)?),min:(*b).min(*y),max:(*c).max(*z)},
   319	        // Only an explicit rename provides nominal identity; structural similarity
   320	        // or independently declared replacement tables cannot justify a bridge.
   321	        (TypeKind::Ref{table:a},TypeKind::Ref{table:b}) if a==b || tables.get(a).is_some_and(|(_,_,t)| t.name==*b)=>TypeKind::Ref{table:a.clone()},
   322	        (a,b) if a==b=>a.clone(),
   323	        // Other conversions require an explicit typed migration. A bridge never
   324	        // guesses a nominal reference, money currency or asset policy.
   325	        _=>return None,
   326	    };
   327	    Some(ast::ScalarType{kind,optional:old.optional||new.optional})
   328	}

//! Column syntax uses the existing read-rule binder and resolved index planner.
// @ref LLP 3000.036#221-surface-and-authority — groups and inline declarations share one payload gate.
use super::{
    catalog::{Catalog, TableInfo},
    schema::bind_rule,
};
use crate::{
    ast::{Effect, RuleDecl},
    diag::{Diagnostic, Site},
};
use snapback4_core::schema::RuleExpr;
use std::collections::BTreeMap;

pub(super) fn refusal(site: &Site, message: impl Into<String>) -> Diagnostic {
    Diagnostic::new(site,"E_COLUMN_READ",message,"keep payload authority on the column; use ordinary public keys for authority, order and derived storage (guide rules)").guide("rules")
}

pub(super) fn bind(
    catalog: &Catalog,
    info: &TableInfo,
    diagnostics: &mut Vec<Diagnostic>,
) -> BTreeMap<String, RuleExpr> {
    let inline = info.decl.columns.iter().filter_map(|c| {
        c.read.as_ref().map(|value| {
            (
                vec![c.name.clone()],
                RuleDecl {
                    effect: Effect::Read,
                    bindings: Vec::new(),
                    value: value.clone(),
                    site: c.site.clone(),
                },
            )
        })
    });
    let mut out = BTreeMap::new();
    for (fields, rule) in inline.chain(info.decl.column_reads.iter().cloned()) {
        let value = match bind_rule(catalog, info, &rule) {
            Ok(rule) => rule,
            Err(error) => {
                diagnostics.push(error);
                continue;
            }
        };
        for field in fields {
            if field == "id" || !info.columns.contains_key(&field) {
                diagnostics.push(refusal(
                    &rule.site,
                    format!(
                        "{}.{} is not an ordinary declared payload column",
                        info.decl.name, field
                    ),
                ));
                continue;
            }
            if out.insert(field.clone(), value.clone()).is_some() {
                diagnostics.push(refusal(
                    &rule.site,
                    format!(
                        "{}.{} has more than one column read rule",
                        info.decl.name, field
                    ),
                ));
            }
        }
    }
    out
}

pub(super) fn notes(schema: &snapback4_core::schema::Schema) -> Vec<Diagnostic> {
    let mut notes = Vec::new();
    for (name, table) in schema.visible_tables() {
        if !table.column_reads.is_empty()
            || schema.maintain_for(name).is_some()
            || table.through.is_some()
        {
            continue;
        }
        let parents: Vec<_> = table
            .columns
            .iter()
            .filter_map(|(field, kind)| match kind {
                snapback4_core::schema::ColumnType::Ref { table: parent } => Some((field, parent)),
                _ => None,
            })
            .collect();
        if let [(field, parent)] = parents.as_slice()
            && table
                .indexes
                .values()
                .any(|i| i.unique && i.columns() == [(*field).clone()])
            && let Some(p) = schema.table(parent)
            && table.rules.read != p.rules.read
            && !table.rules.read.is_uniformly_readable()
        {
            notes.push(Diagnostic::new(&Site::default(),"N_COLUMN_READ",format!("{name} is a one-to-one extension of {parent} with a separate read audience; a field that only narrows visibility can use `field: type read <- policy` on {parent}"),"use a column rule for a private field; keep this table when it represents a separate entity or lifecycle").guide("rule-recipes"));
        }
    }
    notes
}

#[derive(Clone, Debug, PartialEq, Eq)]
pub struct ColumnAggregate {
    pub source: String,
    pub of: Option<String>,
    pub filter: Option<Expr>,
    /// Empty until reference inference in the catalog when `by` is omitted.
    pub by: String,
}

/// `sync public | to .column | to table[.a, .b] [by .principal] [last N by index]`.
#[derive(Clone, Debug, PartialEq, Eq)]
pub struct SyncDecl {
    pub audience: SyncAudience,
    pub horizon: Option<(u32, SyncOrder)>,
    pub own_keys: Option<String>,
    pub site: Site,
}

// @ref LLP 3000.019#3.1 — column intent resolves to the same ordinary index as a named window.
#[derive(Clone, Debug, PartialEq, Eq)]
pub enum SyncOrder {
    Index(String),
    Columns(Vec<String>),
}

impl std::fmt::Display for SyncOrder {
    fn fmt(&self, f: &mut std::fmt::Formatter<'_>) -> std::fmt::Result {
        match self {
            Self::Index(name) => f.write_str(name),
            Self::Columns(columns) => f.write_str(&columns.iter().map(|column| format!(".{column}")).collect::<Vec<_>>().join(", ")),
        }
    }
}

A full positional unique key may come first, followed by named extra-column tests, as in `docs[.docId, ownerId = viewer]`. Every focal field used by the key or extra tests must be pinned for one verdict to serve a whole page. `created t[..]` is valid in insert rules only. A rule cannot vary with time (use `expire`). A read rule that is exactly `exists t[.a, .b, viewer]`, `.col = viewer` or `public` can be synced (guide sync). The system principal consults no rules, so `system` is never a rule word. An audience read rule may add `or viewer = 'service:pusher'` (or several service grants); sync infers its audience without them, and ordinary extra disjuncts still refuse.
201:| Unreadable or absent | `NOT_FOUND` for rule denial, an `update` condition, secondary unique clash, or an incomplete insert patch; no row, index, column or branch detail |
203:Use `upsert counters[recipient] { count: 1 }` under an `update` rule to write another principal's row. Success discloses the admitted row's existence and the update rule predicates. It grants no extra stream or snapshot row. A condition belongs on `update`; an upsert inserts or patches. A condition needs a row you can read; a blind patch needs none. A blind update or upsert-patch needs no read rule: the update rule judges the old and next images; a preceding program lookup still needs read access. On a row you cannot read, immutable columns in a patch are ignored. A readable immutable mismatch still names the column. Before target lookup, the engine reserves room for the rule probes and the largest merged image allowed by the schema and supplied patch; if it cannot fit, `E_BOUND` refuses equally for a hidden or absent target. A readable aggregate view over a hidden source is a declared read channel: its delta can disclose the old contribution. `N_MAINTAIN_DELTA` notes potentially wider update authority under a separately readable aggregate view. Restrict its read audience or source write authority if that delta is private.
288:### Disclosure: aggregates, assets and maintained columns
289:Omitted asset columns keep their placement. An explicit asset id on a row you
314:A maintained column such as `likeCount: count likes by postId` belongs to the engine, while ordinary fields keep their existing writer rules. Source rules authorize contributions; parent readers receive the count, including counts over private sources. Rules and their probes cannot depend on maintained fields. Adding or redefining the column recounts and mirrors live parents without consulting parent write rules; its event-free generation barrier reacquires devices and wakes server-run parent watches. See `guide maintains`.
318:`readUpTo: int default 0 written by .principal` gives that field its own writer: the required `principal` column on the same row. An insert by anyone else omits it and receives the declared default. Supplying even the default explicitly requires ownership. On update and the patch arm of upsert, the old row's owner authorizes each touched owned field. Other touched fields still require the row's update rule. An owner can patch just their fields when the table has no update rule. A named `update (old, next)` rule also constrains owner writes; it can compare matching integers or times with `<`, `<=`, `>` and `>=`. Read rules keep their equality-only contract.
364:E_FIELD_AUTHORITY means an owner declaration is invalid, or a supplied/updated field is not yours. Name a required principal column on this table, declare a default, and omit the field when writing another principal's row. A private field in an index or aggregate needs `readable by audience` (after `written by`) when that disclosure fits. An unreadable update or refused delete target, and an absent upsert refused by insert authority or field ownership, keep `NOT_FOUND` with the same refusal and meter. Direct inserts and readable targets keep `E_FIELD_AUTHORITY`; the system keeps its rule bypass. A page over private fields must "scope this read to a uniformly readable audience" (a walk over hidden rows counts them): `check` refuses a scan its pins do not decide, role guard or not. Pin the read rule's column (`visits where .parent = viewer`), move the field to its own table keyed by the row, or make it `readable by audience`.
371:field on its existing row with `read <- policy` when it only narrows payload
374:N_UNIQUE_HIDDEN names a private unique declaration whose else code applies only to readable conflicts. A hidden conflict keeps generic E_RULE (or an undisclosed target miss). Source advice may quote the declared clause; public refusals never confirm a hidden row. Prefer a minimal readable key projection or operator-mediated claim when availability is public; do not widen private payloads for a diagnostic.

//! Asset/reference renames transport cells, homes and lifetime evidence.
// @ref LLP 3000.000.000.002#8-wave-21-release-evolution — asset identity is preserved; no upload, re-claim or generated placeholder.
use super::*;
use std::collections::BTreeMap;
type Maps=BTreeMap<String,BTreeMap<String,String>>;
fn refusal(message:impl Into<String>)->RuntimeError {RuntimeError::Structural{code:"E_ADOPT",message:message.into()}}
fn replace_keys<T>(fields:BTreeMap<String,T>,map:&BTreeMap<String,String>)->BTreeMap<String,T> {fields.into_iter().map(|(name,value)|(map.get(&name).cloned().unwrap_or(name),value)).collect()}
fn table(table:&mut Table,map:&BTreeMap<String,String>){
    table.columns=replace_keys(std::mem::take(&mut table.columns),map);
    table.defaults=replace_keys(std::mem::take(&mut table.defaults),map);
    table.column_reads=replace_keys(std::mem::take(&mut table.column_reads),map);
    table.on_delete=replace_keys(std::mem::take(&mut table.on_delete),map);
    table.written_by=replace_keys(std::mem::take(&mut table.written_by),map);
    fn term(t:&mut crate::schema::RuleTerm,map:&BTreeMap<String,String>){use crate::schema::RuleTerm::*;if let Field(c)|OldField(c)|NextField(c)|Reference{column:c,..}|ReferenceChain{column:c,..}=t {if let Some(new)=map.get(c){*c=new.clone();}}}
    fn rule(r:&mut crate::schema::RuleExpr,map:&BTreeMap<String,String>){use crate::schema::RuleExpr::*;match r {Eq(a,b)|Ne(a,b)|Compare(_,a,b)=>{term(a,map);term(b,map)},And(xs)|Or(xs)=>for x in xs{rule(x,map)},Not(r)=>rule(r,map),Exists{key,equalities,..}|Created{key,equalities,..}=>{for t in key{term(t,map)}for(_,t)in equalities{term(t,map)}},_=>()}}
    for r in [&mut table.rules.read,&mut table.rules.insert,&mut table.rules.update,&mut table.rules.delete]{rule(r,map)}
    for r in table.column_reads.values_mut(){rule(r,map)}
    for index in table.indexes.values_mut(){for component in &mut index.components {if let crate::schema::IndexComponent::Column(c)=component {if let Some(new)=map.get(c){*c=new.clone();}}}}
    if let Some(sync)=&mut table.sync {
        for audience in std::iter::once(&mut sync.audience).chain(sync.also.iter_mut().map(|a|&mut a.audience)) {
            match audience {crate::schema::Audience::Column(c)=>{if let Some(new)=map.get(c){*c=new.clone();}},crate::schema::Audience::Target{prefix,..}=>for c in prefix {if let Some(new)=map.get(c){*c=new.clone();}},_=>()}
        }
    }
    for c in &mut table.constraints {match c{
        crate::schema::Constraint::Immutable{fields}|crate::schema::Constraint::Cap{by:fields,..}=>for f in fields{if let Some(new)=map.get(f){*f=new.clone();}},
        crate::schema::Constraint::FinalExistsOnInsert{key,..}=>for t in key{term(t,map)},
        _=>(),
    }}
}
// @ref LLP 3000.000.000#20-visible-rollback-views-roll — comparison uses the same typed identity codec as activation.
impl Schema {
    /// Compare schema authority using declared physical column identities.
    pub fn with_column_names(&self,maps:&BTreeMap<String,BTreeMap<String,String>>)->Self{schema(self.clone(),maps)}
}
pub(super) fn schema(mut schema:Schema,maps:&Maps)->Schema {
    for(name,map)in maps {if let Some(t)=schema.tables.get_mut(name){table(t,map);}}
    for e in &mut schema.extractions {
        if let Some(map)=maps.get(&e.child){e.reference=map.get(&e.reference).cloned().unwrap_or_else(||e.reference.clone());e.fields=std::mem::take(&mut e.fields).into_iter().map(|(dest,source)|(map.get(&dest).cloned().unwrap_or(dest),source)).collect();}
        if let Some(map)=maps.get(&e.parent){for source in e.fields.values_mut(){if let Some(new)=map.get(source){*source=new.clone();}}}
    }schema
}
pub(super) fn prepare(conn:&Connection,next:&Schema,catalog:&BTreeMap<String,i64>,renamed_tables:&BTreeMap<String,String>)->Result<Maps,RuntimeError>{
    let exists:bool=conn.query_row("SELECT EXISTS(SELECT 1 FROM sqlite_schema WHERE name='_asset_column_renames')",[],|r|r.get(0))?;
    if !exists && next.asset_renames.is_empty(){return Ok(Maps::new())}
    conn.execute_batch("CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS _asset_column_renames(t INTEGER NOT NULL,name TEXT NOT NULL,origin TEXT NOT NULL,PRIMARY KEY(t,name)) WITHOUT ROWID;")?;
    let mut maps=Maps::new();
    for (name,current) in &next.tables {
        let Some(t)=catalog.get(name) else {continue};
        let mut aliases:BTreeMap<String,String>=conn.prepare("SELECT name,origin FROM _asset_column_renames WHERE t=?1")?.query_map([t],|r|Ok((r.get(0)?,r.get(1)?)))?.collect::<Result<_,_>>()?;
        for (new,old) in next.asset_renames.get(name).into_iter().flatten() {
            if old==new || current.columns.contains_key(old) || !current.columns.get(new).is_some_and(asset){return Err(refusal(format!("asset rename {name}.{old} → {new} requires one predecessor-only source and one asset destination")))}
            let root=aliases.get(old).cloned().unwrap_or_else(||old.clone());
            if aliases.get(new).is_some_and(|r|r!=&root){return Err(refusal("asset rename cannot merge independent column lifetimes"))}
            for c in [old,new] {aliases.insert(c.clone(),root.clone());conn.execute("INSERT OR IGNORE INTO _asset_column_renames VALUES(?1,?2,?3)",params![t,c,root])?;}
        }
        let mut selected=BTreeMap::new();for c in current.columns.keys(){if let Some(root)=aliases.get(c){if selected.insert(root.clone(),c.clone()).is_some(){return Err(refusal("two names for the same asset column cannot be active together"))}}}
        let map:BTreeMap<_,_>=aliases.into_iter().filter_map(|(old,root)|selected.get(&root).filter(|new|**new!=old).map(|new|(old,new.clone()))).collect();
        for(old,new)in &map {
            let prior:Option<String>=conn.query_row("SELECT schema FROM _generations WHERE generation=(SELECT value FROM _meta WHERE key='generation')",[],|r|r.get(0)).optional()?;
            if let Some(prior)=prior {
                let prior:Schema=serde_json::from_str(&prior).map_err(|e|refusal(e.to_string()))?;
                let prior=prior.with_table_names(renamed_tables)?;
                if let Some(before)=prior.table(name).filter(|t|t.columns.contains_key(old)){
                    let source=&before.columns[old];let destination=&current.columns[new];
                    if reference(source)||reference(destination){
                        if source!=destination||before.on_delete.get(old)!=current.on_delete.get(new)||before.through.as_ref().is_some_and(|t|t.reference==*old)||current.through.as_ref().is_some_and(|t|t.reference==*new){return Err(refusal("reference rename requires identical nominal type, deletion policy and uncaptured ownership in both directions"))}
                    }
                }
            }
            for rows in ["r","_dormant_rows","_dormant_narrow_rows"] {
                if !exists_table(conn,rows)?{continue;}
                let a=format!("$.Row.{}",serde_json::to_string(old).unwrap());let b=format!("$.Row.{}",serde_json::to_string(new).unwrap());
                let conflict:bool=conn.query_row(&format!("SELECT EXISTS(SELECT 1 FROM {rows} WHERE t=?1 AND json_type(data,?2) IS NOT NULL AND json_type(data,?3) IS NOT NULL AND json_extract(data,?2)!=json_extract(data,?3))"),params![t,a,b],|r|r.get(0))?;
                if conflict{return Err(refusal(format!("asset rename {name}.{old} → {new} has a conflicting physical destination")))}
                conn.execute(&format!("UPDATE {rows} SET data=json_remove(json_set(data,?3,json_extract(data,?2)),?2) WHERE t=?1 AND json_type(data,?2) IS NOT NULL"),params![t,a,b])?;
            }
            conn.execute("UPDATE _assets SET home_column=?3 WHERE home_table=?1 AND home_column=?2",params![name,old,new])?;
            if exists_table(conn,"_dormant_refs")? {conn.execute("UPDATE _dormant_refs SET column_name=?3 WHERE t=?1 AND column_name=?2",params![t,old,new])?;}
            if exists_table(conn,"_dormant_values")? {conn.execute("UPDATE _dormant_values SET column_name=?3 WHERE t=?1 AND column_name=?2",params![t,old,new])?;}
            if exists_table(conn,"_dormant_columns")? {conn.execute("UPDATE _dormant_columns SET column_name=?3 WHERE table_name=?1 AND column_name=?2",params![name,old,new])?;}
        }
        if !map.is_empty(){
            for rows in ["_dormant_narrow_rows","_dormant_tables"] {
                if !exists_table(conn,rows)?{continue;}
                let condition=if rows=="_dormant_tables"{"table_name=?1"}else{"t=?1"};
                let value=if rows=="_dormant_tables"{rusqlite::types::Value::Text(name.clone())}else{rusqlite::types::Value::Integer(*t)};
                let definitions:Vec<String>=conn.prepare(&format!("SELECT DISTINCT definition FROM {rows} WHERE {condition}"))?.query_map([&value],|r|r.get(0))?.collect::<Result<_,_>>()?;
                for text in definitions {let mut definition:Table=serde_json::from_str(&text).map_err(|e|RuntimeError::Store(e.to_string()))?;table(&mut definition,&map);conn.execute(&format!("UPDATE {rows} SET definition=?2 WHERE {condition} AND definition=?3"),params![value,canonical_json(&definition),text])?;}
            }
            maps.insert(name.clone(),map);
        }
    }
    conn.execute("UPDATE _meta SET value=?1 WHERE key='version'",[super::table_renames::VERSION])?;
    Ok(maps)
}
fn exists_table(conn:&Connection,name:&str)->Result<bool,RuntimeError>{Ok(conn.query_row("SELECT EXISTS(SELECT 1 FROM sqlite_schema WHERE name=?1)",[name],|r|r.get(0))?)}
