//! Table names may change; numeric row, reference and receipt lifetimes do not.
// @ref LLP 3000.000.000.002#8-wave-21-release-evolution — renames are identity moves, never row copies or merges.
use super::*;
use std::collections::BTreeMap;
pub(super) const VERSION:&str="snapback4-release-store-9";
fn refuse(s:impl Into<String>)->RuntimeError {RuntimeError::Structural{code:"E_ADOPT",message:s.into()}}

// Only structural table identifiers change. Literal values, column names,
// operation names and authored defaults are never table identifiers.
fn identifiers(value:&mut serde_json::Value,map:&BTreeMap<String,String>) {
    match value {
        serde_json::Value::Array(xs)=>for x in xs {identifiers(x,map)},
        serde_json::Value::Object(fields)=>for (key,x) in fields {
            if key=="Literal" || key=="defaults" {continue;}
            if matches!(key.as_str(),"table"|"parent") {if let Some(name)=x.as_str().and_then(|s|map.get(s)){*x=serde_json::Value::String(name.clone());continue;}}
            identifiers(x,map);
        },_=>()
    }
}
fn mapped(name:&str,map:&BTreeMap<String,String>)->String {map.get(name).cloned().unwrap_or_else(||name.into())}
fn remap<T:serde::Serialize+serde::de::DeserializeOwned>(value:&T,map:&BTreeMap<String,String>)->Result<T,RuntimeError> {
    let mut json=serde_json::to_value(value).map_err(|e|RuntimeError::Store(e.to_string()))?;identifiers(&mut json,map);
    serde_json::from_value(json).map_err(|e|RuntimeError::Store(e.to_string()))
}
pub(crate) fn schema(mut schema:Schema,map:&BTreeMap<String,String>)->Result<Schema,RuntimeError> {
    if map.is_empty(){return Ok(schema)}
    schema.tables=std::mem::take(&mut schema.tables).into_iter().map(|(name,table)|Ok((mapped(&name,map),remap(&table,map)?))).collect::<Result<_,RuntimeError>>()?;
    for m in &mut schema.maintains {*m=remap(m,map)?;m.source=mapped(&m.source,map);m.target=mapped(&m.target,map);}
    for f in &mut schema.backfills {fill(f,map,false)?;}
    for f in &mut schema.downfills {fill(f,map,true)?;}
    for e in &mut schema.extractions {e.child=mapped(&e.child,map);}
    // Remaining table-bearing structural metadata is normalized through the
    // same typed codec; maintains' source/target are handled explicitly above.
    // These maps contain column identifiers, including arbitrary keys such as
    // "parent". Normalize their typed table slots, never their column strings.
    let mut extractions=std::mem::take(&mut schema.extractions);
    for e in &mut extractions {e.parent=mapped(&e.parent,map);}
    let columns=std::mem::take(&mut schema.asset_renames).into_iter().map(|(name,columns)|(mapped(&name,map),columns)).collect();
    schema.table_renames.clear();schema=remap(&schema,map)?;
    schema.extractions=extractions;schema.asset_renames=columns;
    Ok(schema)
}
fn fill(f:&mut crate::schema::backfill::Backfill,map:&BTreeMap<String,String>,down:bool)->Result<(),RuntimeError> {
    *f=remap(f,map)?;
    f.program.name=format!("{}{}{}",if down{"down"}else{"backfill"},f.table,f.name);Ok(())
}
pub(super) fn prepare(conn:&Connection,next:&Schema,catalog:&mut BTreeMap<String,i64>)->Result<BTreeMap<String,String>,RuntimeError> {
    let exists:bool=conn.query_row("SELECT EXISTS(SELECT 1 FROM sqlite_schema WHERE name='_table_renames')",[],|r|r.get(0))?;
    if !exists && next.table_renames.is_empty(){return Ok(BTreeMap::new())}
    conn.execute_batch("CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS _table_renames(name TEXT PRIMARY KEY,origin TEXT NOT NULL) WITHOUT ROWID;")?;
    let mut names:BTreeMap<String,String>=conn.prepare("SELECT name,origin FROM _table_renames")?.query_map([],|r|Ok((r.get(0)?,r.get(1)?)))?.collect::<Result<_,_>>()?;
    for (new,old) in &next.table_renames {
        if new==old || !next.tables.contains_key(new) || next.tables.contains_key(old){return Err(refuse("table rename requires a distinct predecessor-only source"))}
        let origin=names.get(old).cloned().unwrap_or_else(||old.clone());
        if names.get(new).is_some_and(|root|root!=&origin){return Err(refuse(format!("table rename {old} → {new} conflicts with an independent retained identity")))}
        if let (Some(a),Some(b))=(catalog.get(old),catalog.get(new)) {if a!=b{return Err(refuse(format!("table rename {old} → {new} cannot merge existing table lifetimes")))}}
        for name in [old,new] {names.insert(name.clone(),origin.clone());conn.execute("INSERT OR IGNORE INTO _table_renames VALUES(?1,?2)",params![name,origin])?;}
    }
    let mut selected=BTreeMap::<String,String>::new();
    for name in next.tables.keys() {if let Some(root)=names.get(name) {if let Some(other)=selected.insert(root.clone(),name.clone()){return Err(refuse(format!("table aliases {other} and {name} cannot be active together")))}}}
    let map:BTreeMap<String,String>=names.iter().filter_map(|(name,root)|selected.get(root).filter(|new|*new!=name).map(|new|(name.clone(),new.clone()))).collect();
    for (old,new) in &map {
        if !catalog.contains_key(old){continue;}
        if catalog.contains_key(new){return Err(refuse(format!("table rename {old} → {new} conflicts with retained table storage")))}
        let entries:Vec<_>=catalog.iter().filter(|(key,_)|*key==old || key.starts_with(&format!("{old}."))).map(|(k,v)|(k.clone(),*v)).collect();
        for (key,num) in entries {let renamed=format!("{new}{}",&key[old.len()..]);conn.execute("UPDATE _catalog SET name=?1 WHERE name=?2",params![renamed,key])?;catalog.remove(&key);catalog.insert(renamed,num);}
        conn.execute("UPDATE _assets SET home_table=?1 WHERE home_table=?2",params![new,old])?;
        for table in ["_dormant_tables","_dormant_columns","_dormant_indexes"] {
            if conn.query_row("SELECT EXISTS(SELECT 1 FROM sqlite_schema WHERE name=?1)",[table],|r|r.get::<_,bool>(0))? {conn.execute(&format!("UPDATE {table} SET table_name=?1 WHERE table_name=?2"),params![new,old])?;}
        }
    }
    // Retained typed definitions must use the current public table names too.
    for table in ["_dormant_tables","_dormant_columns","_dormant_narrow_rows"] {
        if !conn.query_row("SELECT EXISTS(SELECT 1 FROM sqlite_schema WHERE name=?1)",[table],|r|r.get::<_,bool>(0))? {continue;}
        let rows:Vec<(String,String)>=conn.prepare(&format!("SELECT definition,definition FROM {table} GROUP BY definition"))?.query_map([],|r|Ok((r.get(0)?,r.get(1)?)))?.collect::<Result<_,_>>()?;
        for (old,text) in rows {let mut json:serde_json::Value=serde_json::from_str(&text).map_err(|e|RuntimeError::Store(e.to_string()))?;identifiers(&mut json,&map);let new=canonical_json(&json);if old!=new {conn.execute(&format!("UPDATE {table} SET definition=?1 WHERE definition=?2"),params![new,old])?;}}
    }
    history(conn,&map)?;
    conn.execute("UPDATE _meta SET value=?1 WHERE key='version'",[VERSION])?;
    Ok(map)
}
fn history(conn:&Connection,map:&BTreeMap<String,String>)->Result<(),RuntimeError> {
    for (table,down) in [("_backfill_definitions",false),("_downfill_definitions",true)] {
        if !conn.query_row("SELECT EXISTS(SELECT 1 FROM sqlite_schema WHERE name=?1)",[table],|r|r.get::<_,bool>(0))? {continue;}
        let rows:Vec<(String,String)>=conn.prepare(&format!("SELECT digest,definition FROM {table}"))?.query_map([],|r|Ok((r.get(0)?,r.get(1)?)))?.collect::<Result<_,_>>()?;
        for (old,text) in rows {let mut f:crate::schema::backfill::Backfill=serde_json::from_str(&text).map_err(|e|RuntimeError::Store(e.to_string()))?;fill(&mut f,map,down)?;let new=if down {f.down_key()}else{f.key()};if old==new{continue;}
            conn.execute(&format!("INSERT OR IGNORE INTO {table} VALUES(?1,?2)"),params![new,canonical_json(&f)])?;
            for ledger in ["_backfill_evidence","_backfill_pruned","_backfill_absence"] {conn.execute(&format!("UPDATE {ledger} SET definition=?1 WHERE definition=?2"),params![new,old])?;}
            conn.execute("UPDATE _backfill_absence SET definition=?1 WHERE definition=?2",params![format!("$native:{new}"),format!("$native:{old}")])?;
            conn.execute("UPDATE _backfill_absence SET definition=?1 WHERE definition=?2",params![format!("$authored:{new}"),format!("$authored:{old}")])?;
            for c in f.inputs.keys() {conn.execute("UPDATE _dormant_refs SET column_name=?1 WHERE column_name=?2",params![format!("$backfill:{new}:{c}"),format!("$backfill:{old}:{c}")])?;}
            conn.execute(&format!("DELETE FROM {table} WHERE digest=?1"),[old])?;
        }
    }Ok(())
}

| `exists t[key]` in a program | false | true when its extra equalities match |

Later requires cannot replace earlier refusals. Defaults can mask hidden reads.
N_LOOKUP_FILTERED names mutation lookups lacking proved read authority, including asserted reads and tables later written; prefer minimal
readable projections. N_LOOKUP_HIDDEN reviews required read/write gates.
Blind writes need no lookup. Rule `exists` uses separate witnesses. See `guide refusals` and local `why recent`.

### Column read rules

A column may append `read <- policy`; `read (a, b) <- policy` on the table shares a gate. Both narrow row authority, omit denied row properties and make
explicit selections optional/null. The complete runnable example and sync, asset, aggregate and rollback boundaries are in `guide rule-recipes`,
Column-level privacy. Row-local cells sync; probe-based cells stay online and ordinary queries fall back automatically. Protected payloads cannot supply
stored keys, cursors/order/search, authority, lifetimes, immutable constraints or maintained derivation inputs (E_COLUMN_READ). Use an ordinary public key
for structure, or a separate entity with its own lifecycle.

### Hops, operators and read through
Write rules and `after insert` keys allow three live hops: `.reaction.message.conversation`, including `exists members[.message.conversation, viewer]`. `messages[id = .message].conversation` is equivalent. Every target must be live and readable; absent, expired or hidden targets remain unknown under `not` and `= null`. Program lookups (`t[id] !`, `exists`) are reads too: a row the caller cannot read is null in an optional lookup (false for program `exists`) and the authored CODE in an asserted lookup, before any later `require` or the delete/update rule runs, so give a moderator read access to what they moderate, or delete by id without a lookup and let the delete rule decide (`guide refusals` has the recipe). `check` notes N_UNREADABLE_PROBE on guards without proved read authority; use unique/cap/after insert constraints for global absence across hidden rows.
Each occurrence prepays one probe, including skipped branches. `check --cost`/`why` list them; prediction needs every read covered.
Insert uses the new image; an unnamed `update <- R` must hold for both the row before and the row after the write (pair `update <- .owner = viewer` with `immutable owner`); a named `update (old, next)` judges exactly what it names; delete uses its pre-delete image; final obligations resolve at transaction end. Read-through capture/lifetimes stay unchanged. Null guards narrow only the same term and image.

pub(super) fn bind(catalog: &Catalog, schema: &mut Schema, diagnostics: &mut Vec<Diagnostic>) {
    for d in catalog.documents.iter().flat_map(|d|&d.extractions) {
        let extraction=snapback4_core::schema::Extraction{child:d.child.clone(),parent:d.parent.clone(),reference:d.reference.clone(),fields:d.fields.iter().cloned().collect()};
        schema.extractions.push(extraction);
    }
    let mut forward_names=BTreeSet::new();let mut forward_outputs=BTreeSet::new();
    for d in catalog.documents.iter().flat_map(|d|&d.backfills) {bind_one(catalog,schema,diagnostics,d,false,&mut forward_names,&mut forward_outputs);}
    let mut down_names=BTreeSet::new();let mut down_outputs=BTreeSet::new();
    for d in catalog.documents.iter().flat_map(|d|&d.downfills) {bind_one(catalog,schema,diagnostics,d,true,&mut down_names,&mut down_outputs);}
    for table in catalog.documents.iter().flat_map(|d|&d.tables) {
        if let Some(old)=&table.renamed_from {
            if old==&table.name || catalog.tables.contains_key(old) || schema.table_renames.values().any(|v|v==old) {
                diagnostics.push(type_error(&table.site,"table rename requires one distinct predecessor-only source","keep one name for each row lifetime"));
            } else {schema.table_renames.insert(table.name.clone(),old.clone());}
        }
        for column in table.columns.iter().filter(|c|c.renamed_from.is_some()) {
            let old=column.renamed_from.as_ref().unwrap();
            if old==&column.name || table.columns.iter().any(|c|&c.name==old) {
                diagnostics.push(type_error(&column.site,format!("rename source {}.{old} must be a distinct predecessor-only column",table.name),"remove the current old column and retain only the renamed declaration"));continue
            }
            fn asset(kind:&ast::TypeKind)->bool {match kind {ast::TypeKind::Image{..}|ast::TypeKind::Video{..}|ast::TypeKind::File{..}=>true,ast::TypeKind::List{item,..}=>asset(&item.kind),_=>false}}
            if (asset(&column.type_.kind)||matches!(&column.type_.kind,ast::TypeKind::Ref{..})) && column.maintained.is_none() && column.sequence.is_none() && column.written_by.is_none() {
                schema.asset_renames.entry(table.name.clone()).or_default().insert(column.name.clone(),old.clone());continue;
            }
            if matches!(&column.type_.kind, ast::TypeKind::Ref{..}|ast::TypeKind::Image{..}|ast::TypeKind::Video{..}|ast::TypeKind::File{..}|ast::TypeKind::List{..})
                || column.maintained.is_some() || column.sequence.is_some() || column.written_by.is_some()
            {
                diagnostics.push(type_error(&column.site,format!("column rename on {}.{} requires an authoritative scalar without reference, asset, collection, captured or lifetime ownership",table.name,column.name),"use a bridge migration that preserves the protected field's authority and lifetime"));continue
            }
            let declaration=|input:&str,output:&str,prefix:&str|ast::BackfillDecl{
                table:table.name.clone(),name:format!("$rename:{prefix}:{old}:{}",column.name),
                args:vec![ast::ArgDecl{alias:None,default:None,deprecated:false,name:input.into(),type_:column.type_.clone(),site:column.site.clone()}],
                value:ast::Expr::Object{fields:vec![ast::ObjectField{spread:false,name:output.into(),value:Some(ast::Expr::Name{name:input.into(),site:column.site.clone()}),site:column.site.clone()}],site:column.site.clone()},site:column.site.clone()
            };
            bind_one(catalog,schema,diagnostics,&declaration(old,&column.name,"up"),false,&mut forward_names,&mut forward_outputs);
            bind_one(catalog,schema,diagnostics,&declaration(&column.name,old,"down"),true,&mut down_names,&mut down_outputs);
        }
    }
}

#[test]
fn protected_scalar_rename_reverse_projection_and_argument_alias_survive_two_release_cycles(){
 let v1="table records:\n  owner: principal\n  body: text <=80\n  legacy: text <=80 ?\n  public\n  insert <- .owner = viewer\n  update <- .owner = viewer\n  online only\nquery one(id: records):\n  return records[id]\nmutation add(reason: text <=80):\n  return insert records {owner: viewer,body: reason,legacy: null}\n";
 let v2="table records:\n  owner: principal\n  secret: text <=80 renamed from body read <- .owner = viewer\n  public\n  insert <- .owner = viewer\n  update <- .owner = viewer\n  online only\nquery one(id: records):\n  return records[id]\nmutation add(explanation: text <=80 alias reason):\n  return insert records {owner: viewer,secret: explanation}\ndown records oldView(secret: text <=80): {legacy:secret}\ninvariant records.secret read only .owner = viewer\n";
 let b=clean(v1);let mut e=Engine::memory(b.schema,b.programs.into_iter().map(|p|p.program)).unwrap();
 let b=clean(v1);e.adopt_release_at(b.schema,b.programs.into_iter().map(|p|p.program),&snapback4_core::engine::release::ReleaseActivation::new(1,None),true,1_800_000_000_000).unwrap();
 for cycle in 0..2 {
  if cycle==0 {
  let b=clean(v2);let mut a=snapback4_core::engine::release::ReleaseActivation::new(2,Some(1));a.restore_dormant=true;
  e.adopt_release_at(b.schema,b.programs.into_iter().map(|p|p.program),&a,true,1_800_000_000_000).unwrap();
  }
  let text=format!("private-{cycle}");let mut ctx=context("alice");ctx.args.insert("reason".into(),Value::String(text.clone()));
  let sent=e.mutate("add",None,ctx).unwrap();let WriteState::Sent{result,..}=sent.state else{panic!("{sent:?}")};let key=result.field("id").unwrap().clone();
  let query=|viewer:&str|{let mut c=context(viewer);c.args.insert("id".into(),key.clone());c};
  assert!(e.query("one",query("bob")).unwrap().data.field("secret").is_err());
  let b=clean(v1);let mut a=snapback4_core::engine::release::ReleaseActivation::new(1,Some(2));a.restore_dormant=true;
  e.adopt_release_at(b.schema,b.programs.into_iter().map(|p|p.program),&a,true,1_800_000_000_000).unwrap();
  let old=e.query("one",query("bob")).unwrap().data;
  assert_eq!(old.field("body").unwrap(),&Value::String(text.clone()));assert_eq!(old.field("legacy").unwrap(),&Value::String(text.clone()));
  let mut ctx=context("alice");ctx.args.insert("reason".into(),Value::String(format!("old-{cycle}")));assert!(matches!(e.mutate("add",None,ctx).unwrap().state,WriteState::Sent{..}));
  let b=clean(v2);let mut a=snapback4_core::engine::release::ReleaseActivation::new(2,Some(1));a.restore_dormant=true;
  e.adopt_release_at(b.schema,b.programs.into_iter().map(|p|p.program),&a,true,1_800_000_000_000).unwrap();
  assert_eq!(e.query("one",query("alice")).unwrap().data.field("secret").unwrap(),&Value::String(text));
  assert!(e.query("one",query("bob")).unwrap().data.field("secret").is_err());
 }
}

#[test]
fn protected_spread_types_preserve_omission_nested_fields_and_fallback_presence(){
 let b=clean(&(SOURCE.to_owned()+"query spread(id: records):\n  r = records[id] ! else MISSING\n  s = {...r}\n  return {nested: {...s}}\nquery fallback(id: records):\n  r = records[id] ! else MISSING\n  return {secret: 'fallback', ...r}\nquery scalarSpread(id: records):\n  r = records[id] ! else MISSING\n  s = {...r}\n  return s.secret\n"));
 let spread=b.programs.iter().find(|p|p.program.name=="spread").unwrap();
 let ty=snapback4_lang::emit::ty_ts(&spread.result);assert!(ty.contains("secret?: string;"),"{ty}");
 let fallback=b.programs.iter().find(|p|p.program.name=="fallback").unwrap();let ty=snapback4_lang::emit::ty_ts(&fallback.result);assert!(ty.contains("secret: string;"),"{ty}");assert!(!ty.contains("secret?:"),"{ty}");
 let scalar=b.programs.iter().find(|p|p.program.name=="scalarSpread").unwrap();assert_eq!(snapback4_lang::emit::ty_ts(&scalar.result),"string | null");
}

//! Bind historical arguments without requiring them in the current row type.
use super::*;
use crate::{ast, diag::{type_error, unknown}, types::{Ty, scalar_to_column}};
use snapback4_core::schema::backfill::Backfill;
use std::collections::{BTreeMap, BTreeSet};

fn bind_one(
    catalog:&Catalog,schema:&mut Schema,diagnostics:&mut Vec<Diagnostic>,d:&ast::BackfillDecl,down:bool,
    names:&mut BTreeSet<(String,String)>,outputs:&mut BTreeSet<(String,String)>,
) {
    let word=if down{"down projection"}else{"backfill"};
    if !names.insert((d.table.clone(),d.name.clone())) { diagnostics.push(unknown(&d.site,format!("duplicate {word} name on this table"),"give each historical transform its own name")); return; }
    let decl = ast::ProgramDecl { delivery: None, held:false, predicts:false, server:false, kind:ast::ProgramKind::Query,
        name: format!("{}{}{}",if down{"down"}else{"backfill"},d.table,d.name), args:d.args.clone(), prelude:vec![],
        body:vec![ast::Stmt::Return { value:d.value.clone(), site:d.site.clone() }], native:false, site:d.site.clone() };
    let Some(bound) = program::Binder::bind_historical(catalog,schema,"schema",&decl,diagnostics) else {return};
    let Ty::Object(fields) = &bound.result else { diagnostics.push(type_error(&d.site,format!("{word} returns an object patch"),"{ column: historicalValue }")); return; };
    let Some(table) = schema.table(&d.table) else {diagnostics.push(unknown(&d.site,format!("{word} table is not declared"),"declare the current table"));return};
    for (c,ty) in fields {
        if !down {if let Some(kind) = table.columns.get(c) {
            if !ty.assignable_to(&Ty::from_column(kind)) { diagnostics.push(type_error(&d.site,format!("backfill {}.{c} returns {}, expected {}",d.table,ty.name(),kind),"return a value fitting the candidate column")); }
        }}
        if !outputs.insert((d.table.clone(),c.clone())) { diagnostics.push(type_error(&d.site,format!("{word}s overlap on {}.{c}",d.table),"combine simultaneous outputs in one declaration")); }
    }
    let fill = Backfill { table:d.table.clone(), name:d.name.clone(), inputs:d.args.iter().map(|a|(a.name.clone(),scalar_to_column(&a.type_))).collect::<BTreeMap<_,_>>(), outputs:fields.keys().cloned().collect(), program:bound.program };
    let admitted=if down {fill.admit_down(schema)}else{fill.admit(schema)};
    if let Err(why) = admitted { diagnostics.push(type_error(&d.site,why,"use deterministic typed fields and a scalar patch").guide("deploy")); }
    if down {schema.downfills.push(fill.normalized())}else{schema.backfills.push(fill.normalized())}
}

fn endpoint_contracts(old:&crate::Bound,new:&crate::Bound,pause_removed_writes:bool)->bool {
    old.programs.iter().all(|p|new.programs.iter().find(|n|n.program.name==p.program.name)
        .map_or_else(||pause_removed_writes&&matches!(p.program.kind,snapback4_core::ir::ProgramKind::Mutation|snapback4_core::ir::ProgramKind::Job|snapback4_core::ir::ProgramKind::Action),|n|endpoint_contract(&p.program,&n.program)))
}
pub(super) fn endpoint_contract(old:&snapback4_core::Program,new:&snapback4_core::Program)->bool {
    if old.kind!=new.kind{return false;}
    let mut positions=BTreeSet::new();

    }

**MATERIAL: compiler field-authority comparison still falsely refuses declared column renames.**

Concrete predecessor `schema.q`:

```quarry
table items:
  owner: principal
  secret: text <=20 read <- .owner = viewer
  n: int
  public
  online only
query legacyRead(id: items):
  return items[id]
```

Current source makes three changes:

- Rename `secret` to `detail: text <=20 renamed from secret read <- .owner = viewer`.
- Widen `n` to `int ?`.
- Rename `legacyRead` to `currentRead`, keeping its arguments and body identical.

The selective bridge can retain `legacyRead`, widen `n`, and preserve the identical payload guard under the injective identity `detail → secret`. This involves no changed helper, invented default, selection or permission.

However, [release_bridge.rs:211](/home/ccheever/projects/snapback-sb4loop/snapback4/crates/snapback4-lang/src/release_bridge.rs:211) aligns only table names. At [line 221](/home/ccheever/projects/snapback-sb4loop/snapback4/crates/snapback4-lang/src/release_bridge.rs:221), it compares the view’s `column_reads["secret"]` against current `column_reads.get("secret")`, which is absent because current uses `detail`. It therefore emits “rollback view needs current field authority on items.”

Neither fallback rescues this: complete-current requires predecessor column spellings, and guarded-current rejects the missing `legacyRead` query through [endpoint_contracts](/home/ccheever/projects/snapback-sb4loop/snapback4/crates/snapback4-lang/src/release_bridge_current.rs:135). Retaining a predecessor query after its current name changes is already supported by [the selective-bridge regression](/home/ccheever/projects/snapback-sb4loop/snapback4/crates/snapback4-lang/src/release_bridge.rs:381).

CLI admission applies `with_column_names` at [rollback_views.rs:200](/home/ccheever/projects/snapback-sb4loop/snapback4/crates/snapback4/src/verbs/rollback_views.rs:200), but preparation fails before reaching it. The compiler comparison needs the same injective column alignment.

Source-traced finding; no edits, builds or tests performed.
